# WS-01 Dossier: Grounding, Taxonomy Reconciliation & Platform Fact Baseline

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-01` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Target Runtime:** CPython `3.12.13` (`vLLM 0.19.1`, `google-adk`) | **Companion Artifacts:** [`taxonomy_baseline.json`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/taxonomy_baseline.json), [`contradiction_log.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/contradiction_log.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Scope

Workstream `WS-01` establishes the authoritative, reconciled fact baseline for the **Google Gemma 4 Developer Agent Competition** (`https://www.kaggle.com/competitions/gemma-4-developer-agent/`) and freezes the canonical 106-group, 10-dimension taxonomy in `taxonomy_baseline.json`. All downstream workstreams (`WS-02` through `WS-16`) inherit their group IDs, task counts, runtime pins, and hardware budgets from this dossier.

---

## 2. Resolution of the Four Taxonomy Count Discrepancies (`D1`–`D4`)

Prior workspace deliverables contained four internal count discrepancies (`D1`–`D4` in Handoff §6.4). Cross-auditing `scratch/2026-11-05_representative_issues_research/enriched_129_with_graphs.json`, `taxonomy_knowledge_base.py`, `02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md`, and `05_Quality_Check_and_Table_Validation_Report.md` resolves all four with exact arithmetic (`MEASURED`):

| Discrepancy ID | Prior Conflicting Statements | Authoritative Resolution (`taxonomy_baseline.json`) | Root Cause of Prior Discrepancy | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `D1` | 02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md Section 1 table (line 17) and 00_README (line 61) state '22 (RC-1A to RC-8B)', whereas Section 3.2 (lines 86-110) of the exact same file enumerates 25 distinct RC-* subcategories summing to 129. | 25 distinct Axis 2B Root-Cause Sub-Categories (RC-1A..1C [3], RC-2A..2C [3], RC-3A..3D [4], RC-4A..4C [3], RC-5A..5D [4], RC-6A..6C [3], RC-7A..7C [3], RC-8A..8B [2] = 25). The '22' in the Section 1 summary cell was a copy-paste clerical error from the task count of RC-1 (22 tasks). | Clerical summary table mismatch vs enumerated sections | `MEASURED` |
| `D2` | 02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md line 3 headline states '48 primary categories/classes and 57 fine-grained sub-categories' (= 105), while Section 1 table rows sum to 36 primary and 61 sub (= 97). | 106 distinct taxonomic groups across 10 orthogonal groupings: Axis 1A (6) + Axis 1B (17) + Axis 2A (8) + Axis 2B (25) + Axis 3 (18) + Axis 4 (5) + Axis 5A (5) + Axis 5B (4) + Axis 6 (8) + Evolutionary Multi-PR Chains (10) = 106. The headline '48 + 57 = 105' had included 4 repo-level totals + 10 chains on the primary side (36 - 4 + 10 + 6 = 48) and miscounted RC-* subcategories by 3; the canonical 10-dimension decomposition is 106. | Clerical summary table mismatch vs enumerated sections | `MEASURED` |
| `D3` | 05_Quality_Check_and_Table_Validation_Report.md Table 2.1 lists 11 rows whose group counts are 6, 17, 8, 25, 18, 5, 5, 4, 8, 10, and 129. | Rows 1-10 of Table 2.1 are the 10 taxonomic group dimensions summing to exactly 6+17+8+25+18+5+5+4+8+10 = 106 groups. Row 11 ('Complete 129-Task Master Catalog', 129 rows) is the individual task-level catalog, not a taxonomic group dimension. | Clerical summary table mismatch vs enumerated sections | `MEASURED` |

## 3. Verified Competition Platform, Runtime & Leaderboard Facts (`RQ-07`–`RQ-10`)

All competition environment constants below are verified from `input/kagglecomp/kagglecomp_data_package/HARNESS_README.md`, `input/kagglecomp/2026-11-05_executive_summary_discussion_threads.md`, and live zip inspection (`empirical_results.json`):

| Parameter / Subsystem | Verified Specification | Source / Thread ID | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Evaluation Split & Ceiling** | `129` public training tasks (`fastapi`: 67, `rich`: 48, `requests`: 13, `httpx`: 1); `~58` tasks on Public LB (`1` task = `0.01724` score); `~120` hidden tasks from private repos on Private LB. `11` public tasks fail in uncalibrated sandbox (`fastapi_14186`, `rich_3486`, `rich_3472`, and 8 `requests` tasks), capping raw public ceiling at `~0.91`. | `HARNESS_README.md`, `#755554`, `#754586` | `MEASURED` |
| **Leaderboard Standing (2026-11-05)** | `4,808` teams; `4,375` (`90.99%`) at `0.00`. Rank 1: **Yurnero** (`0.29`, `17/58` tasks, confirmed LoRA viability). Ranks 2–10 tied at `0.24` (`14/58` tasks). Bronze cutoff: `0.01` (Rank 433). | `00-intel-brief-2026-11-05.md` | `MEASURED` |
| **Prize Tracks & Deadlines** | Main LB: `$125,000` (Top 5: `$50k`/`$30k`/`$20k`/`$15k`/`$10k`). Small Model Bonus: `$10,000` (`#751104`, top Private LB on E2B/E4B among the 2 final submission slots). Paper Track (`3,000` words max, due `2026-11-12`): `$35,000` (`$15k` Best Paper, `$10k` New Resource, `$10k` New Application). Final submission deadline: `2026-12-02 23:59 UTC`. | `HARNESS_README.md`, `#751104`, `3-cc-opus55` | `DOCUMENTED` |
| **Single-Variant Hard Rule** | Mixing model variants within a single submission (e.g. 31B root + E4B sub-agent) is strictly forbidden and flagged by the submission validator. Each submission zip must use **one** model variant across all agents. | `#750016` (Staff: Ashley Chow) | `DOCUMENTED` |
| **Scorer Hardware & Runtime** | 4x NVIDIA L4 GPUs (`96 GB` VRAM total), 12-hour (`720 min`) total wall-clock limit (`~5.58 min/task` for 129 tasks; `~6.0 min/task` for 120 tasks), CPython `3.12.13`, `vLLM 0.19.1` (`--enable-auto-tool-choice --tool-call-parser gemma4 --reasoning-parser gemma4`), `google-adk`, `network_mode='none'`. | `HARNESS_README.md`, `#755291` | `DOCUMENTED` |
| **Two-Container Isolation** | **Container A (Agent Sandbox):** `FAIL_TO_PASS` and `PASS_TO_PASS` stripped from `/workspace/task.json`; git remotes scrubbed; wiped after every task. **Container B (Scorer Verifier):** fresh container applying final `git diff` + `test_patch` and running `FAIL_TO_PASS` and `PASS_TO_PASS` (`resolved = 1` iff 100% pass). | `HARNESS_README.md` §3–4 | `DOCUMENTED` |
| **Pre-Computed AST Graph Reality** | `127` graph JSON files (`graphs/<repo>_<commit>.json`) and `127` `.npz` embedding files. Nodes have **only** `['id', 'name', 'text']` (no file path, no line numbers); edges have **only** `type='calls'`. Crucial defect: **0 `async def` nodes** exist across all `367,985` nodes (`21–23` tasks touch `async def`), and `fastapi` graphs have `72%` isolated nodes (`3,061/4,263`). | `empirical_results.json`, `04-deep-dive` | `MEASURED` |

---

## 4. The Eight Fatal Execution Killers in the Competition Environment

| # | Killer ID & Thread | Subsystem | Exact Failure Mechanism | Mandatory BCF v3 Mitigation | Evidence Tier |
| ---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | **vLLM Array Parser Infinite Loop (`#753973`)** | `vLLM 0.19.1` (`gemma4` parser) | `_parse_gemma4_array` enters an infinite `while` loop when a string element inside a tool array argument contains an unescaped `]` (common in `get_code_subgraph(nodes: list[str])`), ballooning RAM to `26.7 GB` and freezing vLLM for all remaining tasks (`0.00` score). | **Never invoke `get_code_subgraph`** (`allow_get_code_subgraph: false` in both 31B and E4B profiles). | `REPORTED` / `DOCUMENTED` |
| 2 | **`edit_file` Backtick Key Swallowing (`#756051`)** | `vLLM` + `edit_file` tool | When `new_string` or `old_string` contains triple backticks (```` ``` ````), the Gemma 4 tool parser swallows the `,old_string:` delimiter into the string body, causing `edit_file` to fail `51` consecutive times in a single task. | **Forbid backticks in `edit_file`**; when editing Markdown/docstrings containing backticks, write the edit via a 5-line `python -c "from pathlib import Path..."` command in `run_command`. | `REPORTED` |
| 3 | **ADK Compaction History Wipeout (`#754783`)** | `google.adk` `LlmEventSummarizer` | At the default `14,336`-token compaction threshold, `LlmEventSummarizer` checks only `if part.text:`, silently dropping 100% of `function_call` and `function_response` parts and causing complete agent amnesia. | Raise `compaction_threshold_tokens` to `24,000`–`28,000` and cap `max_turns` (`40` on 31B, `28` on E4B) + `read_file` chunk size (`<=150` lines) so traces finish before compaction. | `REPORTED` |
| 4 | **ADK `SequentialAgent` Continuation Loop (`#754636`)** | `google.adk` `SequentialAgent` | When a sub-agent stops early, the harness continuation nudge (`'Please continue working on the task...'`) routes back to `sub_agents[0]` (the locator), re-running localization and burning `10k–12k` tokens. | Use a **Single Flat `LlmAgent`** (`root_agent_class: LlmAgent`, `use_sub_agents: false`). | `REPORTED` |
| 5 | **PEFT `Gemma4ClippableLinear` & LoRA Token Inflation (`#755677`)** | `PEFT` + `vLLM` LoRA | Stock `peft` raises `ValueError` on `Gemma4ClippableLinear` wrappers unless unwrapped to `.linear`. Active LoRA adapters inflate token generation by `+45%` and latency by `+29%`, blowing the 12h timeout under default 10-min budgets. | Unwrap `Gemma4ClippableLinear` before `get_peft_model()`, cap `max_time_minutes: 5.5` (31B LoRA) / `4.5` (E4B LoRA), and set `thinking_budget: 2048` (31B) / `1024` (E4B). | `REPORTED` |
| 6 | **Decode Throughput Bottleneck & `read_file` Truncation (`#755291`)** | `vLLM` + `read_file` | Decode is `17.25 tok/s` on 31B (`76%` of wall-clock time is decode; prefill is `1.71 s/turn`). `read_file` silently truncates at `150` lines / `10,000` chars. | Keep thinking concise, always pass `start_line` and `end_line` (`<=150` lines) to `read_file`, and use `rg -n` to find exact line numbers first. | `MEASURED` / `REPORTED` |
| 7 | **E4B QAT 4-Bit Trailing Character Drop (`#753277`)** | `gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct` | 4-bit QAT quantization causes E4B to occasionally drop the final character of a tool argument string (`old_string` or file path), causing `edit_file` mismatch or syntax error. | Run `python -m py_compile <file> && git diff` immediately after every edit on E4B, and set `temperature: 0.0`. | `REPORTED` |
| 8 | **0-Async-Node AST Graph Blind Spot** | Pre-computed `graphs/*.json` | `0` `async def` functions are indexed across all `367,985` graph nodes (`sample_async_def_nodes = 0` in `empirical_results.json`), making `21–23` async tasks invisible to graph tools. | Mandatory `rg -n 'async def '` lexical search (`SR-5`) before trusting any negative graph result. | `MEASURED` |

# WS-02 Dossier: Python Triage Surface for `RC-1` — Type System, Introspection & Callable Unwrapping

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-02` | **Root-Cause Family:** `RC-1` (`22/129` tasks, `17.05%`) | **Target Runtime:** CPython `3.12.13` (`3.10`–`3.15` annotated) | **Machine Artifact:** [`rc1_mechanisms.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/rc1_mechanisms.jsonl).

## 1. Executive Summary & Taxonomic Scope

This dossier documents the authoritative Python language, standard-library, and third-party triage surface for **`RC-1: Type System, Introspection & Callable Unwrapping`** (`22` benchmark tasks, `17.05%` of the 129-task corpus) and its fine-grained subcategories (`RC-1A`, `RC-1B`, `RC-1C`), together with cross-mapped primary subcategories and evolutionary chains (`SUB-1A`, `SUB-2B`, `CHAIN-02`, `CHAIN-04`, `CHAIN-05`, `CHAIN-06`).

## 2. Authoritative Python Triage Surface & Version Matrix (`rc1_mechanisms.jsonl`)

| Mechanism ID | Subcategory | Python / Library Symbols | Version Floor & Ceiling | Correct Idiom | Incorrect Idiom (Anti-Pattern) | Observable Failure Signature | Verification Command |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC1-M01` | `RC-1A` | `typing.get_origin / typing.get_args / types.UnionType / typing.Union` | `3.8 (get_origin/get_args), 3.10 (types.UnionType PEP 604)` → `3.15+` | `origin = get_origin(ann); is_union = origin is Union or origin is types.UnionType` | origin = getattr(ann, '__origin__', None); is_union = (origin is Union) | TypeError or HTTP 422 Unprocessable Content on `list[UploadFile] \| None` or discriminated union inside `Annotated[Union[A, B], Field(discriminator=...)]`. | `pytest -k 'test_union or test_annotated or test_upload_file' -q` |
| `RC1-M02` | `RC-1A` | `typing.Annotated / typing.TypeAliasType (PEP 695)` | `3.9 (Annotated PEP 593), 3.12 (TypeAliasType PEP 695)` → `3.15+` | `while isinstance(ann, TypeAliasType): ann = ann.__value__; if get_origin(ann) is Annotated: base, *meta = get_args(ann)` | Assuming `get_origin(alias)` unwraps `TypeAliasType` automatically or stripping `Annotated` metadata before Pydantic `FieldInfo` extraction. | AssertionError / FastAPIError ('Cannot use Query() for type...') or ignored `Field(discriminator=...)` when wrapped in `type MyParam = Annotated[str, Query()]`. | `pytest -k 'test_type_alias or test_annotated' -q` |
| `RC1-M03` | `RC-1B` | `inspect.unwrap / functools.partial / inspect.isclass / inspect.iscoroutinefunction` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Loop unwrapping `isinstance(c, functools.partial) -> c.func` and `inspect.unwrap(c)`, stopping immediately if `inspect.isclass(c)` before descending into `c.__call__`.` | Single-pass `_impartial(inspect.unwrap(c))` or descending into `c.__call__` when `inspect.isclass(c)` is True. | TypeError ('object X can't be used in await expression') when a dependency is a class type defining `async def __call__`, or sync threadpool execution of async generator wrapped in `partial`. | `pytest -k 'test_unwrap or test_partial or test_callable_class' -q` |
| `RC1-M04` | `RC-1B` | `typing.runtime_checkable / Protocol / inspect.getattr_static vs hasattr` | `3.12` → `3.15+` | `def has_read(obj: Any) -> bool: return isinstance(obj, SupportsRead) or hasattr(obj, 'read')` | isinstance(obj, SupportsRead) or isinstance(obj, Iterable) alone on objects delegating methods via `__getattr__`. | AttributeError or silent misrouting in `requests.PreparedRequest.prepare_body` / `_encode_files` when passed `NamedTemporaryFile` or custom file-like proxy on Python 3.12+. | `pytest tests/test_requests.py -k 'TemporaryFile or file_wrapper' -q` |
| `RC1-M05` | `RC-1B` | `inspect.Parameter.empty / dataclasses.fields / __qualname__ / __reduce__` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `if param.default is param.empty: ...; val = getattr(obj, field.name, MISSING); qual = getattr(obj, '__qualname__', None); isinstance(qual, str)` | if param.default == param.empty: ...; getattr(obj, field.name) without default on `init=False` dataclass; calling `.rsplit()` on unvalidated `__qualname__` descriptor. | TypeError on custom `__eq__` (`rich_3006`), AttributeError on uninitialized `@dataclass` `field(init=False)` (`rich_3472`), AttributeError on non-str `__qualname__` (`rich_3894`), TypeError pickling `requests.exceptions.JSONDecodeError` (`requests_6629`). | `pytest -k 'test_repr or test_pretty or test_inspect or test_json_decode_error' -q` |
| `RC1-M06` | `RC-1C` | `inspect.signature(call, eval_str=True) / inspect.get_annotations / typing.get_type_hints` | `3.10 (eval_str=True in inspect.signature and inspect.get_annotations)` → `3.15+` | `globalns = getattr(inspect.unwrap(call), '__globals__', None); try: sig = inspect.signature(call, globals=globalns, eval_str=True) except (NameError, TypeError): sig = inspect.signature(call, eval_str=False)` | Calling `inspect.signature(call, eval_str=True)` without `try/except NameError` (crashes on `if TYPE_CHECKING:` imports) or without passing unwrapped `__globals__` (crashes on `@functools.wraps` across modules). | NameError: name 'SomeTypeCheckingOnlyClass' is not defined at route registration time, or unresolved string forward reference `'CustomDep'` treated as query string parameter. | `pytest -k 'test_type_checking or test_future_annotations or test_wrapped_dependency' -q` |

### 2.1 Detailed CPython Version Delta Analysis (`3.10` → `3.15`)

| Mechanism ID | Version | Language / Stdlib Change & Triage Impact | Official Documentation Reference |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC1-M01` | **Python `3.10`** | PEP 604 introduces `X \| Y` creating `types.UnionType`; `get_origin(int \| str)` returns `types.UnionType` (`!= typing.Union`). | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin) |
| `RC1-M01` | **Python `3.12`** | PEP 695 introduces `typing.TypeAliasType`; `get_origin(alias)` returns `None` until unwrapped via `alias.__value__`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin) |
| `RC1-M01` | **Python `3.14`** | `typing.Union` and `types.UnionType` unified in `typing` internals, but `get_origin` checks must still support `(Union, UnionType)` on 3.10-3.13. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin) |
| `RC1-M02` | **Python `3.12`** | Adds `type Alias = ...` creating `typing.TypeAliasType` with lazy `. __value__` and `.__type_params__`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.TypeAliasType](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.TypeAliasType) |
| `RC1-M02` | **Python `3.13`** | `typing_extensions.TypeAliasType` backports PEP 695 behavior for 3.10-3.11. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.TypeAliasType](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.TypeAliasType) |
| `RC1-M03` | **Python `3.10`** | `inspect.iscoroutinefunction` unwraps `@functools.wraps` via `inspect.unwrap`, and in 3.10+ supports `functools.partial`, but does not unwrap interleaved `partial(wraps(partial(...)))` or guard class types defining `async def __call__`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.unwrap](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.unwrap) |
| `RC1-M03` | **Python `3.12`** | `inspect.markcoroutinefunction` added for explicit coroutine marking. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.unwrap](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.unwrap) |
| `RC1-M04` | **Python `3.11`** | `@runtime_checkable` `Protocol.__instancecheck__` used `hasattr(instance, attr)`, invoking dynamic `__getattr__`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.getattr_static](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.getattr_static) |
| `RC1-M04` | **Python `3.12`** | CPython gh-102433 switched `typing._ProtocolMeta.__instancecheck__` to `inspect.getattr_static(instance, attr)`, which bypasses `__getattr__` and returns `None` on proxy objects like `tempfile._TemporaryFileWrapper`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.getattr_static](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.getattr_static) |
| `RC1-M05` | **Python `3.10`** | `inspect.Parameter.empty` is a singleton sentinel class; `dataclasses.fields(obj)` returns all declared `Field` objects regardless of whether `init=False` attributes have been set on `obj`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.Parameter.empty](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.Parameter.empty) |
| `RC1-M06` | **Python `3.10`** | Adds `inspect.get_annotations(obj, *, globals=None, locals=None, eval_str=False)` and `eval_str` parameter to `inspect.signature`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.signature](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.signature) |
| `RC1-M06` | **Python `3.14`** | PEP 649 / PEP 749 introduces lazy `__annotate__` functions and `annotationlib.get_annotations(obj, format=Format.VALUE\|FORWARDREF\|STRING)`. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.signature](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.signature) |

---

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC1-M01` (`RC-1A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_12942`, `fastapi_14512`, `fastapi_14297`, `fastapi_14430`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `typing.get_origin / typing.get_args / types.UnionType / typing.Union` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.get_origin))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** TypeError or HTTP 422 Unprocessable Content on `list[UploadFile] | None` or discriminated union inside `Annotated[Union[A, B], Field(discriminator=...)]`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** origin = getattr(ann, '__origin__', None); is_union = (origin is Union)
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `origin = get_origin(ann); is_union = origin is Union or origin is types.UnionType`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_union or test_annotated or test_upload_file' -q`

### 4.2 `RC1-M02` (`RC-1A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_13920`, `fastapi_12942`, `fastapi_14512`, `fastapi_14794`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `typing.Annotated / typing.TypeAliasType (PEP 695)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/typing.html#typing.TypeAliasType))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** AssertionError / FastAPIError ('Cannot use Query() for type...') or ignored `Field(discriminator=...)` when wrapped in `type MyParam = Annotated[str, Query()]`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Assuming `get_origin(alias)` unwraps `TypeAliasType` automatically or stripping `Annotated` metadata before Pydantic `FieldInfo` extraction.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `while isinstance(ann, TypeAliasType): ann = ann.__value__; if get_origin(ann) is Annotated: base, *meta = get_args(ann)`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_type_alias or test_annotated' -q`

### 4.3 `RC1-M03` (`RC-1B`) — Flagships: `fastapi_9555`, `fastapi_9753`, `fastapi_14448`, `fastapi_14458`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `inspect.unwrap / functools.partial / inspect.isclass / inspect.iscoroutinefunction` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.unwrap))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** TypeError ('object X can't be used in await expression') when a dependency is a class type defining `async def __call__`, or sync threadpool execution of async generator wrapped in `partial`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Single-pass `_impartial(inspect.unwrap(c))` or descending into `c.__call__` when `inspect.isclass(c)` is True.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Loop unwrapping `isinstance(c, functools.partial) -> c.func` and `inspect.unwrap(c)`, stopping immediately if `inspect.isclass(c)` before descending into `c.__call__`.`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_unwrap or test_partial or test_callable_class' -q`

### 4.4 `RC1-M04` (`RC-1B`) — Flagships: `requests_7433`, `requests_7502`, `requests_7505`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `typing.runtime_checkable / Protocol / inspect.getattr_static vs hasattr` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.getattr_static))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** AttributeError or silent misrouting in `requests.PreparedRequest.prepare_body` / `_encode_files` when passed `NamedTemporaryFile` or custom file-like proxy on Python 3.12+.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** isinstance(obj, SupportsRead) or isinstance(obj, Iterable) alone on objects delegating methods via `__getattr__`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `def has_read(obj: Any) -> bool: return isinstance(obj, SupportsRead) or hasattr(obj, 'read')`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_requests.py -k 'TemporaryFile or file_wrapper' -q`

### 4.5 `RC1-M05` (`RC-1B`) — Flagships: `rich_3006`, `rich_3472`, `rich_3894`, `requests_6629`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `inspect.Parameter.empty / dataclasses.fields / __qualname__ / __reduce__` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.Parameter.empty))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** TypeError on custom `__eq__` (`rich_3006`), AttributeError on uninitialized `@dataclass` `field(init=False)` (`rich_3472`), AttributeError on non-str `__qualname__` (`rich_3894`), TypeError pickling `requests.exceptions.JSONDecodeError` (`requests_6629`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** if param.default == param.empty: ...; getattr(obj, field.name) without default on `init=False` dataclass; calling `.rsplit()` on unvalidated `__qualname__` descriptor.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `if param.default is param.empty: ...; val = getattr(obj, field.name, MISSING); qual = getattr(obj, '__qualname__', None); isinstance(qual, str)`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_repr or test_pretty or test_inspect or test_json_decode_error' -q`

### 4.6 `RC1-M06` (`RC-1C`) — Flagships: `fastapi_11355`, `fastapi_14485`, `fastapi_5077`, `fastapi_9425`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `inspect.signature(call, eval_str=True) / inspect.get_annotations / typing.get_type_hints` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/inspect.html#inspect.signature))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** NameError: name 'SomeTypeCheckingOnlyClass' is not defined at route registration time, or unresolved string forward reference `'CustomDep'` treated as query string parameter.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Calling `inspect.signature(call, eval_str=True)` without `try/except NameError` (crashes on `if TYPE_CHECKING:` imports) or without passing unwrapped `__globals__` (crashes on `@functools.wraps` across modules).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `globalns = getattr(inspect.unwrap(call), '__globals__', None); try: sig = inspect.signature(call, globals=globalns, eval_str=True) except (NameError, TypeError): sig = inspect.signature(call, eval_str=False)`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_type_checking or test_future_annotations or test_wrapped_dependency' -q`


---

| `RC2-M03` | **Python `3.11`** | PEP 654 `ExceptionGroup` + PEP 678 `BaseException.add_note()` expand exception traversal from a linear linked list (`__cause__`/`__context__`) to a directed graph requiring `_visited: set[int]` cycle detection. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/traceback.html#traceback.TracebackException](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/traceback.html#traceback.TracebackException) |

```json
  "fixed_escape_backslash": "foo\\\\[/red]"
```

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC2-M01` (`RC-2A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_15588`, `fastapi_14978`, `requests_7205`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Protocol Boundary Single-Line & Media-Type Validators (_check_single_line, Content-Type, netrc)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/netrc.html#netrc.netrc.authenticators))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** CRLF frame injection in SSE streams (`fastapi_15588`), CSRF bypass via `text/plain` JSON POST (`fastapi_14978`), or bogus `Authorization: Basic Og==` header from empty `.netrc` (`requests_7205`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Checking only `'\0' in value` on SSE `event`/`id` fields (`fastapi_15588`), or checking truthiness of tuple `if auth:` when `auth == ('', '', '')` (`requests_7205`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `if any(c in value for c in ('\n', '\r', '\0')): raise ValueError(...); if auth and any(auth): ...`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_sse or test_strict_content_type or test_netrc' -q`

### 4.2 `RC2-M02` (`RC-2B`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14986`, `fastapi_14786`, `rich_3471`, `rich_3278`, `rich_3063`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `json.dumps / str.strip / re.sub Control-Code & Trailing-Backslash Sanitization` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/json.html#json.dumps))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Reflected XSS in Swagger UI (`fastapi_14986`), corrupted span offsets from `'\r'` (`rich_3471`), literal `'7'`/`'8'` from DEC `\x1b7` sequences (`rich_3278`), or unclosed markup tag (`rich_3063`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Raw f-string interpolation `f"url: '{openapi_url}'"` in HTML/JS templates; leaving trailing `'\\'` unescaped before closing markup tag `[/red]`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Use `json.dumps(url)` when embedding URLs into JS strings (`fastapi_14986`); `credentials.strip()` on HTTP auth headers (`fastapi_14786`); `strip_control_codes(content)` in `Text.append_tokens` (`rich_3471`); append `'\\'` if `escaped.endswith('\\') and not escaped.endswith('\\\\')` (`rich_3063`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_swagger_ui or test_append_tokens or test_ansi or test_escape' -q`

### 4.3 `RC2-M03` (`RC-2C`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14258`, `rich_3772`, `fastapi_14479`, `fastapi_14306`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Identity Cycle Guard (seen: set[int] / id(obj)) & PEP 678 Traceback Enrichment` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/traceback.html#traceback.TracebackException))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Infinite loop / MemoryError on `router.include_router(router)` (`fastapi_14258`) or `RecursionError` in `rich.traceback.Traceback.extract` on cyclic exception chains (`rich_3772`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Unbounded `while exc is not None: exc = exc.__cause__ or exc.__context__` or `self.routes.extend(router.routes)` when `router is self`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `if router is self: raise FastAPIError('Cannot include a router into itself'); if id(exc) in visited: break; visited.add(id(exc))`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_include_router_self or test_cyclic_exception or test_endpoint_context' -q`

## 2. Authoritative Python Triage Surface & Version Matrix (`rc3_mechanisms.jsonl`)

| Mechanism ID | Subcategory | Python / Library Symbols | Version Floor & Ceiling | Correct Idiom | Incorrect Idiom (Anti-Pattern) | Observable Failure Signature | Verification Command |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC3-M01` | `RC-3A` | `Pydantic FieldInfo Alias Resolution (field.alias vs validation_alias vs field.name)` | `3.10 (Pydantic v2.0+)` → `3.15+` | `key = get_validation_alias(field) # returns field.field_info.validation_alias if isinstance(..., str) else (field.alias or field.name); track both raw key and underscore-converted key in `processed_keys`.` | Hardcoding `field.alias` when extracting from `request.query_params` / `headers` or failing to record the original hyphenated header key when `convert_underscores=True`. | HTTP 422 missing parameter when `validation_alias` is used inside `Annotated[QueryModel, Query()]` (`fastapi_15589`), or duplicate/leaked extra header fields (`fastapi_14360`, `fastapi_14371`). | `pytest -k 'test_validation_alias or test_header_model or test_empty_form' -q` |
| `RC3-M02` | `RC-3B` | `Starlette ImmutableMultiDict.getlist(key) vs Scalar Extraction & Per-Field Body Dispatch` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Use `received_params.getlist(key)` for sequence fields and extra multi-value params (`extra='allow'`), except when `_is_json_field(field)` is True (`Json[list[str]]` expects scalar JSON string); check `isinstance(field.field_info, params.File)` per field rather than `body_fields[0]`.` | Calling `.get(key)` on extra query/form fields (`fastapi_14356`, `fastapi_14303`), calling `.getlist(key)` on `Json[list[str]]` (`fastapi_14616`), or branching on `isinstance(body_fields[0].field_info, File)` (`fastapi_11194`). | Silent loss of repeated query/form parameters, HTTP 422 `JSON input should be str, bytes or bytearray` on `Query(Json[list[str]])`, or HTTP 422 when `Form` is declared before `File`. | `pytest -k 'test_json_list or test_form_and_file or test_extra_query or test_extra_form' -q` |
| `RC3-M03` | `RC-3C` | `copy.deepcopy / list.copy / Cache & Hash Invalidation (_hash = None)` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Use `copy.deepcopy(response)` when merging OpenAPI response schemas (`fastapi_14463`); iterate over `text._spans.copy()` when `text is self` (`rich_3480`); set `style._hash = None` when mutating cloned `Style` attributes (`rich_2943`); copy `list(on_startup)` (`fastapi_14873`).` | Using `response.copy()` on nested dicts; `self._spans.extend(text._spans)` when `text is self`; copying cached `style._hash = self._hash` after clearing `_meta`/`_link`. | Duplicate `$ref` entries inside OpenAPI `anyOf` (`fastapi_14463`), infinite loop in `Text.append(self)` (`rich_3480`), wrong dict/set lookup due to stale `Style.__hash__` (`rich_2943`), or `Response.history` containing the final response itself (`requests_7328`). | `pytest -k 'test_openapi_responses or test_text_append_self or test_clear_meta or test_redirect_history' -q` |
| `RC3-M04` | `RC-3D` | `Explicit Keyword Argument Plumbing Across Call Chains (DC-05)` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Pass `scope=depends.scope` in `get_parameterless_sub_dependant` (`fastapi_14301`), `openapi_external_docs=self.openapi_external_docs` in `FastAPI.openapi()` (`fastapi_13713`), `markup=True` in `PromptBase.on_validate_error` (`rich_3882`), and `highlight=self.highlight` in `Table.add_row` (`rich_3518`).` | Accepting a configuration keyword on a high-level class/function (`FastAPI.__init__`, `Depends(scope=...)`, `Table(highlight=...)`) and omitting it at an internal helper delegation site. | Silent reversion to default parameter behavior (`scope=None`, `highlight=False`, unstyled markup tags in validation errors). | `pytest -k 'test_paramless_depends_scope or test_external_docs or test_prompt_validate_markup or test_table_highlight' -q` |

### 2.1 Detailed CPython Version Delta Analysis (`3.10` → `3.15`)

| Mechanism ID | Version | Language / Stdlib Change & Triage Impact | Official Documentation Reference |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC3-M01` | **Python `3.12`** | In Pydantic v2, `FieldInfo.validation_alias` may be a `str`, `AliasPath`, or `AliasChoices`, distinct from `FieldInfo.alias` and `ModelField.name`. | [https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/concepts/alias/](https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/concepts/alias/) |
| `RC3-M02` | **Python `3.12`** | Starlette `QueryParams` and `FormData` implement `ImmutableMultiDict`: `d[k]` returns only the last value for key `k`, whereas `d.getlist(k)` returns `list[Any]` of all values. | [https://www.starlette.io/requests/#query-parameters](https://www.starlette.io/requests/#query-parameters) |
| `RC3-M03` | **Python `3.12`** | `dict.copy()` and `copy.copy()` copy only top-level references; mutating nested dicts (`response['content'][media_type]['schema']`) or iterating over `self._spans` while appending to `self` corrupts shared state. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/copy.html#copy.deepcopy](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/copy.html#copy.deepcopy) |
| `RC3-M04` | **Python `3.12`** | Static signature inspection (`inspect.signature`) can verify keyword parameter presence between wrapper and callee. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/expressions.html#calls](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/expressions.html#calls) |

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC3-M01` (`RC-3A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_15589`, `fastapi_14360`, `fastapi_14371`, `fastapi_13537`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Pydantic FieldInfo Alias Resolution (field.alias vs validation_alias vs field.name)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/concepts/alias/))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** HTTP 422 missing parameter when `validation_alias` is used inside `Annotated[QueryModel, Query()]` (`fastapi_15589`), or duplicate/leaked extra header fields (`fastapi_14360`, `fastapi_14371`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Hardcoding `field.alias` when extracting from `request.query_params` / `headers` or failing to record the original hyphenated header key when `convert_underscores=True`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `key = get_validation_alias(field) # returns field.field_info.validation_alias if isinstance(..., str) else (field.alias or field.name); track both raw key and underscore-converted key in `processed_keys`.`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_validation_alias or test_header_model or test_empty_form' -q`

### 4.2 `RC3-M02` (`RC-3B`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14616`, `fastapi_11194`, `fastapi_14356`, `fastapi_14303`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Starlette ImmutableMultiDict.getlist(key) vs Scalar Extraction & Per-Field Body Dispatch` ([Official Docs](https://www.starlette.io/requests/#query-parameters))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Silent loss of repeated query/form parameters, HTTP 422 `JSON input should be str, bytes or bytearray` on `Query(Json[list[str]])`, or HTTP 422 when `Form` is declared before `File`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Calling `.get(key)` on extra query/form fields (`fastapi_14356`, `fastapi_14303`), calling `.getlist(key)` on `Json[list[str]]` (`fastapi_14616`), or branching on `isinstance(body_fields[0].field_info, File)` (`fastapi_11194`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Use `received_params.getlist(key)` for sequence fields and extra multi-value params (`extra='allow'`), except when `_is_json_field(field)` is True (`Json[list[str]]` expects scalar JSON string); check `isinstance(field.field_info, params.File)` per field rather than `body_fields[0]`.`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_json_list or test_form_and_file or test_extra_query or test_extra_form' -q`

### 4.3 `RC3-M03` (`RC-3C`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14463`, `fastapi_5624`, `rich_3480`, `rich_2943`, `rich_3470`, `requests_7328`, `fastapi_15745`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `copy.deepcopy / list.copy / Cache & Hash Invalidation (_hash = None)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/copy.html#copy.deepcopy))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Duplicate `$ref` entries inside OpenAPI `anyOf` (`fastapi_14463`), infinite loop in `Text.append(self)` (`rich_3480`), wrong dict/set lookup due to stale `Style.__hash__` (`rich_2943`), or `Response.history` containing the final response itself (`requests_7328`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Using `response.copy()` on nested dicts; `self._spans.extend(text._spans)` when `text is self`; copying cached `style._hash = self._hash` after clearing `_meta`/`_link`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Use `copy.deepcopy(response)` when merging OpenAPI response schemas (`fastapi_14463`); iterate over `text._spans.copy()` when `text is self` (`rich_3480`); set `style._hash = None` when mutating cloned `Style` attributes (`rich_2943`); copy `list(on_startup)` (`fastapi_14873`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_openapi_responses or test_text_append_self or test_clear_meta or test_redirect_history' -q`

### 4.4 `RC3-M04` (`RC-3D`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14301`, `fastapi_13713`, `rich_3882`, `rich_3518`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Explicit Keyword Argument Plumbing Across Call Chains (DC-05)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/expressions.html#calls))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Silent reversion to default parameter behavior (`scope=None`, `highlight=False`, unstyled markup tags in validation errors).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Accepting a configuration keyword on a high-level class/function (`FastAPI.__init__`, `Depends(scope=...)`, `Table(highlight=...)`) and omitting it at an internal helper delegation site.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Pass `scope=depends.scope` in `get_parameterless_sub_dependant` (`fastapi_14301`), `openapi_external_docs=self.openapi_external_docs` in `FastAPI.openapi()` (`fastapi_13713`), `markup=True` in `PromptBase.on_validate_error` (`rich_3882`), and `highlight=self.highlight` in `Table.add_row` (`rich_3518`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_paramless_depends_scope or test_external_docs or test_prompt_validate_markup or test_table_highlight' -q`


---

### 2.1 Detailed CPython Version Delta Analysis (`3.10` → `3.15`)

| Mechanism ID | Version | Language / Stdlib Change & Triage Impact | Official Documentation Reference |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC4-M01` | **Python `3.12`** | OpenAPI 3.1.0 adopts JSON Schema 2020-12: binary `bytes` payloads use `{'type': 'string', 'contentMediaType': 'application/octet-stream'}` instead of OpenAPI 3.0 `format: binary`. | [https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.1.0](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.1.0) |
| `RC4-M02` | **Python `3.12`** | Python 3.12 `http.HTTPStatus(422).phrase` is `'Unprocessable Content'` per RFC 9110 §15.5.21 (Starlette 0.48+ deprecates `HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY`). CPython `bpo-39057` (`urllib.request.proxy_bypass_environment`) enforces leading dot boundary in `no_proxy`. | [https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html) |
| `RC4-M03` | **Python `3.12`** | No CPython version delta; governed by SPDX License List v3.25+ (`'Apache-2.0'`) and OpenAPI 3.1.0 `License.identifier`. | [https://spdx.org/licenses/Apache-2.0.html](https://spdx.org/licenses/Apache-2.0.html) |

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC4-M01` (`RC-4A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14349`, `fastapi_14361`, `fastapi_13207`, `fastapi_14246`, `fastapi_14953`, `fastapi_14455`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `OpenAPI 3.1.0 & JSON Schema 2020-12 $ref Remapping, @computed_field & Binary Media Types` ([Official Docs](https://spec.openapis.org/oas/v3.1.0))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** AttributeError: `'dict' object has no attribute 'split'` (`fastapi_14349`), `KeyError: '$ref'` (`fastapi_14361`), or missing `@computed_field` in response OpenAPI schema (`fastapi_13207`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Assuming `v` is always a `str` whenever `k == '$ref'` (fails when a Pydantic model defines a field literally named `ref: int = Field(alias='$ref')`, where `properties['$ref']` is a `dict`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Guard `if k == '$ref' and isinstance(v, str):` when rewriting JSON Schema `$ref` strings (`fastapi_14349`); check `if '$ref' in schema:` before indexing (`fastapi_14361`); include `field.computed_fields` in input schema when `separate_input_output_schemas=False` (`fastapi_13207`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_dollar_ref or test_computed_field or test_separate_input_output or test_bytes_schema' -q`

### 4.2 `RC4-M02` (`RC-4B`) — Flagships: `requests_7427`, `requests_7315`, `requests_6644`, `requests_7309`, `requests_6589`, `fastapi_13786`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `RFC 9110 / RFC 3986 HTTP Semantics, URI Double-Slash Preservation & Domain Suffix Matching` ([Official Docs](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** False proxy bypass on `notexample.com` (`requests_7427`), AWS S3 `403 SignatureDoesNotMatch` on `//` keys (`requests_7315`), truncated multi-byte UTF-8 body (`requests_6589`), or RFC 9110 `403` vs `401` mismatch (`fastapi_13786`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Using `host.endswith(domain)` without a dot boundary (`notexample.com` matches `example.com`); stripping leading `//` via `url.lstrip('/')` (`requests_6644` regression reverted by `requests_7315`); using `len(str_body)` (code point count) for HTTP `Content-Length`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `In `proxy_bypass_environment`, match `host == domain or host.endswith('.' + domain.lstrip('.'))` (`requests_7427`); preserve leading `//` in `HTTPAdapter.request_url` path so AWS SigV4 canonical URI matches (`requests_7315`); return `HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED` (not `403`) on missing credentials (`fastapi_13786`); encode `str` to UTF-8 bytes (`len(o.encode('utf-8'))`) in `super_len` (`requests_6589`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_proxy_bypass or test_double_slash or test_super_len or test_security_401' -q`

### 4.3 `RC4-M03` (`RC-4C`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14492`, `fastapi_14487`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `SPDX License Identifier & Safe Error Tutorial Constants in docs_src/` ([Official Docs](https://spdx.org/licenses/Apache-2.0.html))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** AssertionError in `tests/test_tutorial/test_metadata/` or `tests/test_tutorial/test_handling_errors/`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Using non-standard `'MIT'` or `'Apache 2.0'` (with space) inside the `identifier` field where SPDX requires `'Apache-2.0'`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `license_info={'name': 'Apache 2.0', 'identifier': 'Apache-2.0'} (`fastapi_14492`); omit raw `exc.body` echoing in validation error tutorial when demonstrating safe production error responses (`fastapi_14487`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_tutorial/test_metadata/ tests/test_tutorial/test_handling_errors/ -q`

| `RC5-M03` | `RC-5C` | `str.splitlines() vs re.split(r'(?<=\n)', ...) & RFC 3986 URL Regex Character Classes` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Use `re.split(r'(?<=\n)', text)` (or `text.split('\n')` handling trailing newline) when decoding ANSI streams so trailing blank lines are preserved (`rich_4076`); include `@` and `~` in `ReprHighlighter` URL path regex (`rich_3454`, `rich_3067`).` | Using `text.splitlines()` in `AnsiDecoder.decode(text)` (silently drops trailing newline `'a\nb\n' -> ['a', 'b']`, whereas `'a\nb'` also produces `['a', 'b']`). | Dropped trailing blank lines in `AnsiDecoder.decode` (`rich_4076`), ignored `end` parameter on empty `console.print(end='X')` (`rich_4075`), or broken URL highlighting on `https://example.com/~user` and `https://example.com/@user`. | `pytest tests/test_ansi.py tests/test_highlighter.py tests/test_console.py -q` |
| `RC5-M04` | `RC-5D` | `Terminal 2D Box/Padding Geometry & Live Display State Machines` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Subtract horizontal padding `padding_left + padding_right` from available `code_width` in `Syntax` (`rich_3782`); check `if self._live_render.last_render_height:` before emitting newline in `Live.stop()` (`rich_3934`); replace `assert self.body is not None` with `if self.body is not None:` for streaming Markdown tables (`rich_3064`).` | Wrapping `Syntax` lines at `max_width` before adding horizontal padding; using `assert` statements for runtime data presence in streaming parsers. | Truncated right-hand characters in padded `Syntax` blocks (`rich_3782`), spurious blank line when stopping an unrendered `Live` context (`rich_3934`), swapped `mid`/`foot` table borders (`rich_2725`), or `AssertionError` on streaming Markdown tables (`rich_3064`). | `pytest tests/test_syntax.py tests/test_live.py tests/test_table.py tests/test_markdown.py -q` |

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC5-M01` (`RC-5A`) — Flagships: `rich_3930`, `rich_4006`, `rich_3953`, `rich_3944`, `rich_3180`, `rich_3521`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `UAX #29 Grapheme Cluster Segmentation, UAX #11 East Asian Width & Bisect Interval Lookup` ([Official Docs](https://www.unicode.org/reports/tr29/))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Infinite loop (`FM-4`) on `Console().print('\u0301abc')` (`rich_4006`), `IndexError` on solo `'\u200d'` (`rich_3953`), overlapping Nerd Font icons (`rich_3944`), or ragged right margins on CJK tables (`rich_3180`, `rich_3521`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Returning `0` for codepoints above the narrow table (`rich_3944`), allowing `cell_size == 0` to stall the slice index in `chop_cells` (`rich_4006`), or using `len(s)` instead of `cell_len(s)` for CJK/emoji strings.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `In `get_character_cell_size`, return `0` for C0/C1 control codes (`< 32` or `0x7f <= cp < 0xa0`), `1` when `cp > table[-1][1]` (Nerd Fonts PUA, `rich_3944`), guard index bounds on trailing ZWJ `\u200d` (`rich_3953`), and ensure `split_graphemes` / `chop_cells` advances cursor by at least 1 codepoint on leading zero-width characters (`rich_4006`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_cells.py tests/test_segment.py tests/test_wrap.py -q`

### 4.2 `RC5-M02` (`RC-5B`) — Flagships: `rich_4079`, `rich_3130`, `rich_3938`, `rich_3718`, `rich_3296`, `rich_3061`, `rich_3105`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Rich Style / _Span Composition, Background Bleed Prevention & Tab Offset Shifting` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/stdtypes.html#str.expandtabs))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Visual/style corruption (`FM-5`): background color bleeding across terminal width after newline, lost inline code styling in Markdown tables, or misaligned bold/color spans after tab characters.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Applying `style` across the trailing newline `Segment('\n', style)` (causes terminal SGR background color to bleed to the end of the next line on soft wrap, `rich_3938`); replacing `'\t'` in `self._text` without updating `self._spans` (`rich_3061`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Preserve child inline spans in `TableDataElement.on_text` without overwriting base style (`rich_4079` fixing `rich_3130`); exclude trailing `'\n'` segment from background `Style` when `soft_wrap=True` (`rich_3938`); compose `self.style + title_text.style` on `Panel` titles (`rich_3718`); shift `_Span(start, end, style)` offsets as each `'\t'` is expanded in `Text.expand_tabs` (`rich_3061`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_markdown.py tests/test_console.py tests/test_panel.py tests/test_text.py -q`

### 4.3 `RC5-M03` (`RC-5C`) — Flagships: `rich_4076`, `rich_4075`, `rich_3469`, `rich_3454`, `rich_3067`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `str.splitlines() vs re.split(r'(?<=\n)', ...) & RFC 3986 URL Regex Character Classes` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/stdtypes.html#str.splitlines))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Dropped trailing blank lines in `AnsiDecoder.decode` (`rich_4076`), ignored `end` parameter on empty `console.print(end='X')` (`rich_4075`), or broken URL highlighting on `https://example.com/~user` and `https://example.com/@user`.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Using `text.splitlines()` in `AnsiDecoder.decode(text)` (silently drops trailing newline `'a\nb\n' -> ['a', 'b']`, whereas `'a\nb'` also produces `['a', 'b']`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Use `re.split(r'(?<=\n)', text)` (or `text.split('\n')` handling trailing newline) when decoding ANSI streams so trailing blank lines are preserved (`rich_4076`); include `@` and `~` in `ReprHighlighter` URL path regex (`rich_3454`, `rich_3067`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_ansi.py tests/test_highlighter.py tests/test_console.py -q`

### 4.4 `RC5-M04` (`RC-5D`) — Flagships: `rich_3934`, `rich_3935`, `rich_3782`, `rich_2725`, `rich_3064`, `rich_3905`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Terminal 2D Box/Padding Geometry & Live Display State Machines` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-assert-statement))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Truncated right-hand characters in padded `Syntax` blocks (`rich_3782`), spurious blank line when stopping an unrendered `Live` context (`rich_3934`), swapped `mid`/`foot` table borders (`rich_2725`), or `AssertionError` on streaming Markdown tables (`rich_3064`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Wrapping `Syntax` lines at `max_width` before adding horizontal padding; using `assert` statements for runtime data presence in streaming parsers.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Subtract horizontal padding `padding_left + padding_right` from available `code_width` in `Syntax` (`rich_3782`); check `if self._live_render.last_render_height:` before emitting newline in `Live.stop()` (`rich_3934`); replace `assert self.body is not None` with `if self.body is not None:` for streaming Markdown tables (`rich_3064`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/test_syntax.py tests/test_live.py tests/test_table.py tests/test_markdown.py -q`

## 2. Authoritative Python Triage Surface & Version Matrix (`rc6_mechanisms.jsonl`)

| Mechanism ID | Subcategory | Python / Library Symbols | Version Floor & Ceiling | Correct Idiom | Incorrect Idiom (Anti-Pattern) | Observable Failure Signature | Verification Command |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC6-M01` | `RC-6A` | `warnings.warn / UserWarning Subclassing / PEP 702 @warnings.deprecated` | `3.10 (warnings), 3.13 (warnings.deprecated PEP 702; typing_extensions.deprecated on 3.10-3.12)` → `3.15+` | `class FastAPIDeprecationWarning(UserWarning): pass; warnings.warn('...', FastAPIDeprecationWarning, stacklevel=2); @deprecated('Use ... instead') on deprecated response classes (`fastapi_14964`).` | Inheriting custom framework deprecation warnings from `DeprecationWarning` when tests/users expect default visibility without `-W default`, or omitting `stacklevel`. | `Failed: DID NOT WARN` in `pytest.warns(FastAPIDeprecationWarning)` (`fastapi_14605`, `fastapi_14583`, `fastapi_14964`). | `pytest -k 'test_deprecation or test_orjson or test_ujson' -q` |
| `RC6-M02` | `RC-6B` | `Upstream Dependency Shims (Starlette 0.48+ / 1.0, Pydantic 2.12+ / Python 3.14, urllib3 1.x vs 2.x)` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Isolate conditional `pydantic.v1` imports into a dedicated module (`may_v1.py`, `fastapi_14186`); replace `status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY` with literal `422` (`fastapi_14077`); wrap `on_startup`/`on_shutdown` into a composite `lifespan` context manager in `APIRouter` for Starlette 1.0 (`fastapi_14851`).` | Importing `pydantic.v1` unconditionally at top-level startup or delegating `on_event` to `starlette.routing.Router` after Starlette removed `on_event`. | Unexpected `DeprecationWarning` on import (`fastapi_14186`, `fastapi_14077`), `TypeError` in `APIRouter(on_startup=...)` (`fastapi_14851`), or `urllib3 1.26.x` `super_len` encoding mismatch (`requests_6757`). | `pytest -k 'test_on_event or test_422 or test_super_len' -q` |
| `RC6-M03` | `RC-6C` | `Multi-File Compatibility Layer & Dead-Code Surgical Removal (pydantic.v1 Sunset)` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Remove `fastapi/_compat/v1.py` and `may_v1.py`, collapse `_compat` dispatch to v2 direct calls, update `__all__`, and verify `rg 'v1' fastapi/` has zero residual references (`fastapi_14609`).` | Deleting compatibility modules without removing conditional `isinstance(model, v1.BaseModel)` branches or leaving untracked backup files in `/workspace`. | `ImportError: cannot import name 'v1' from 'fastapi._compat'` or failing deprecation removal tests. | `pytest tests/ -k 'not v1' -q` |

### 2.1 Detailed CPython Version Delta Analysis (`3.10` → `3.15`)

| Mechanism ID | Version | Language / Stdlib Change & Triage Impact | Official Documentation Reference |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `RC6-M01` | **Python `3.10`** | Default `warnings.filters` ignores `DeprecationWarning` outside `__main__`, whereas `UserWarning` subclasses are displayed by default. | [https://docs.python.org/3.13/library/warnings.html#warnings.deprecated](https://docs.python.org/3.13/library/warnings.html#warnings.deprecated) |
| `RC6-M01` | **Python `3.13`** | PEP 702 adds `@warnings.deprecated` to the standard library (`typing_extensions.deprecated` used on 3.10-3.12). | [https://docs.python.org/3.13/library/warnings.html#warnings.deprecated](https://docs.python.org/3.13/library/warnings.html#warnings.deprecated) |
| `RC6-M02` | **Python `3.14`** | Pydantic 2.12+ emits deprecation/compatibility warnings when `pydantic.v1` is imported on Python 3.14 (`fastapi_14186`). | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.asynccontextmanager](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.asynccontextmanager) |
| `RC6-M03` | **Python `3.12`** | Use `git rm` and `rg 'pydantic\.v1\|_compat\.v1\|may_v1'` to guarantee zero dangling imports across all 20 files. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-import-statement](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-import-statement) |

## 3. Live Empirical CPython Verification (`empirical_results.json`)

The mechanisms in this dossier were executed and verified against live CPython in `scratch/2026-11-05_gemma4_python_triage_bcf_brain/run_empirical_verifications.py` (`MEASURED`):


---

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC6-M01` (`RC-6A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14605`, `fastapi_14583`, `fastapi_14964`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `warnings.warn / UserWarning Subclassing / PEP 702 @warnings.deprecated` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.13/library/warnings.html#warnings.deprecated))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** `Failed: DID NOT WARN` in `pytest.warns(FastAPIDeprecationWarning)` (`fastapi_14605`, `fastapi_14583`, `fastapi_14964`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Inheriting custom framework deprecation warnings from `DeprecationWarning` when tests/users expect default visibility without `-W default`, or omitting `stacklevel`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `class FastAPIDeprecationWarning(UserWarning): pass; warnings.warn('...', FastAPIDeprecationWarning, stacklevel=2); @deprecated('Use ... instead') on deprecated response classes (`fastapi_14964`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_deprecation or test_orjson or test_ujson' -q`

### 4.2 `RC6-M02` (`RC-6B`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14851`, `fastapi_14186`, `fastapi_14077`, `requests_6757`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Upstream Dependency Shims (Starlette 0.48+ / 1.0, Pydantic 2.12+ / Python 3.14, urllib3 1.x vs 2.x)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.asynccontextmanager))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Unexpected `DeprecationWarning` on import (`fastapi_14186`, `fastapi_14077`), `TypeError` in `APIRouter(on_startup=...)` (`fastapi_14851`), or `urllib3 1.26.x` `super_len` encoding mismatch (`requests_6757`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Importing `pydantic.v1` unconditionally at top-level startup or delegating `on_event` to `starlette.routing.Router` after Starlette removed `on_event`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Isolate conditional `pydantic.v1` imports into a dedicated module (`may_v1.py`, `fastapi_14186`); replace `status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY` with literal `422` (`fastapi_14077`); wrap `on_startup`/`on_shutdown` into a composite `lifespan` context manager in `APIRouter` for Starlette 1.0 (`fastapi_14851`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_on_event or test_422 or test_super_len' -q`

### 4.3 `RC6-M03` (`RC-6C`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14609`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Multi-File Compatibility Layer & Dead-Code Surgical Removal (pydantic.v1 Sunset)` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-import-statement))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** `ImportError: cannot import name 'v1' from 'fastapi._compat'` or failing deprecation removal tests.
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Deleting compatibility modules without removing conditional `isinstance(model, v1.BaseModel)` branches or leaving untracked backup files in `/workspace`.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Remove `fastapi/_compat/v1.py` and `may_v1.py`, collapse `_compat` dispatch to v2 direct calls, update `__all__`, and verify `rg 'v1' fastapi/` has zero residual references (`fastapi_14609`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest tests/ -k 'not v1' -q`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `PEP 657 Fine-Grained Error Locations (co_positions), PEP 678 (__notes__), & os.environ TTY Hooks` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/reference/datamodel.html#codeobject.co_positions))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** Missing caret underline spans or missing `[Note]` blocks in `rich.traceback`, or `FileProxy.isatty()` returning `False` when wrapping a real TTY.

| `RC8-M01` | `RC-8A` | `Pydantic v2 Rust TypeAdapter.dump_json Fast-Path & Function-Local Deferred Imports` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `When `response_model` is a Pydantic v2 model and `response_class is JSONResponse`, serialize directly via `field.serialize_json(...)` returning `Response(content=content_bytes, media_type='application/json')` (`fastapi_14962`); move heavy stdlib/subpackage imports (`inspect`, `mydatetime`, `pygments`, `html`) inside the methods that use them (`rich_4070`).` | Running Python-level `jsonable_encoder` dict traversal before `JSONResponse.render` when a compiled Pydantic v2 `TypeAdapter` already validated the object. | 2x slower JSON serialization throughput (`fastapi_14962`) or 1.5x-1.75x slower `import rich` CLI startup time (`rich_4070`). | `pytest -k 'test_serialize_response or test_lazy_import' -q` |
| `RC8-M02` | `RC-8B` | `contextlib.AsyncExitStack Scope Splitting, BrokenPipeError Devnull Redirect & Keep-Alive Stream Draining` | `3.10` → `3.15+` | `Split `AsyncExitStack` into `request_stack` (closed after response body is sent) and `function_stack` (closed after endpoint function returns) so `yield` dependencies stay open during `StreamingResponse` (`fastapi_14099`); drain unread request body (`while self._parser.recv_body(): ...`) before `self._parser.reset()` in both `src/httpx/` and `src/ahttpx/` (`httpx_3672`); catch `BrokenPipeError`, redirect `sys.stdout = open(os.devnull, 'w')`, and re-raise (`rich_3468`); wrap `with read_image() as img:` (`fastapi_15023`).` | Exiting the request `AsyncExitStack` immediately when `endpoint()` returns a `StreamingResponse` (closes DB sessions and `UploadFile`s before the streaming generator iterates, `fastapi_14099`); resetting `HTTPParser` on keep-alive connection with unread request bytes remaining (`httpx_3672`). | `ValueError: I/O operation on closed file` inside `StreamingResponse` (`fastapi_14099`), `HTTPProtocolError` on second keep-alive request (`httpx_3672`), or unhandled `BrokenPipeError` on `python app.py \| head -n 1` (`rich_3468`). | `pytest -k 'test_streaming_dependency or test_keepalive_unread or test_broken_pipe' -q` |

| `RC8-M02` | **Python `3.12`** | `contextlib.AsyncExitStack` and `contextlib.aclosing` manage async generator teardown deterministically. | [https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.AsyncExitStack](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.AsyncExitStack) |

## 4. Deep-Dive Forensic Walkthroughs on Flagship Competition Tasks

### 4.1 `RC8-M01` (`RC-8A`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14962`, `rich_4070`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `Pydantic v2 Rust TypeAdapter.dump_json Fast-Path & Function-Local Deferred Imports` ([Official Docs](https://docs.pydantic.dev/latest/api/type_adapter/#pydantic.type_adapter.TypeAdapter.dump_json))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** 2x slower JSON serialization throughput (`fastapi_14962`) or 1.5x-1.75x slower `import rich` CLI startup time (`rich_4070`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Running Python-level `jsonable_encoder` dict traversal before `JSONResponse.render` when a compiled Pydantic v2 `TypeAdapter` already validated the object.
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `When `response_model` is a Pydantic v2 model and `response_class is JSONResponse`, serialize directly via `field.serialize_json(...)` returning `Response(content=content_bytes, media_type='application/json')` (`fastapi_14962`); move heavy stdlib/subpackage imports (`inspect`, `mydatetime`, `pygments`, `html`) inside the methods that use them (`rich_4070`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_serialize_response or test_lazy_import' -q`

### 4.2 `RC8-M02` (`RC-8B`) — Flagships: `fastapi_14099`, `httpx_3672`, `rich_3468`, `fastapi_15023`

- **Primary Symbol Surface:** `contextlib.AsyncExitStack Scope Splitting, BrokenPipeError Devnull Redirect & Keep-Alive Stream Draining` ([Official Docs](https://docs.python.org/3.12/library/contextlib.html#contextlib.AsyncExitStack))
- **Observable Symptom / Failure Mode:** `ValueError: I/O operation on closed file` inside `StreamingResponse` (`fastapi_14099`), `HTTPProtocolError` on second keep-alive request (`httpx_3672`), or unhandled `BrokenPipeError` on `python app.py | head -n 1` (`rich_3468`).
- **Why Naive Agents Fail:** Exiting the request `AsyncExitStack` immediately when `endpoint()` returns a `StreamingResponse` (closes DB sessions and `UploadFile`s before the streaming generator iterates, `fastapi_14099`); resetting `HTTPParser` on keep-alive connection with unread request bytes remaining (`httpx_3672`).
- **Canonical BCF v3 Repair Idiom:** `Split `AsyncExitStack` into `request_stack` (closed after response body is sent) and `function_stack` (closed after endpoint function returns) so `yield` dependencies stay open during `StreamingResponse` (`fastapi_14099`); drain unread request body (`while self._parser.recv_body(): ...`) before `self._parser.reset()` in both `src/httpx/` and `src/ahttpx/` (`httpx_3672`); catch `BrokenPipeError`, redirect `sys.stdout = open(os.devnull, 'w')`, and re-raise (`rich_3468`); wrap `with read_image() as img:` (`fastapi_15023`).`
- **Deterministic Verification Recipe:** `pytest -k 'test_streaming_dependency or test_keepalive_unread or test_broken_pipe' -q`

# WS-10 Dossier: Data Structures & Algorithms (DSA) for Python Root-Cause Analysis & Triage

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-10` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Target Runtime:** CPython `3.12.13` | **Machine Artifact:** [`dsa_algorithms.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/dsa_algorithms.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Workstream `WS-10` catalogs the 9 core data structures and algorithms (`DSA-01` through `DSA-09`) that govern both **(a) agent-side fault localization and patch verification** (call-graph ego-networks, AST dominance, Myers diff, delta debugging `ddmin`, and Möbius hunk-interaction algebra) and **(b) target-library algorithmic defects** (binary search on Unicode code-point interval tables, 1D half-open `_Span` interval shifting, object-graph cycle detection, and hash-table cache invariants).

---

## 3. Empirical AST Graph Schema & Reachability Audit (`DSA-01`)

Direct inspection of `input/kagglecomp/kagglecomp_data_package/gemma-4-developer-agent.zip` (`127` graph JSON files and `127` `.npz` embedding files) establishes the exact structural properties of the harness code graphs (`MEASURED`):

| Metric / Property | Measured Value | Operational Impact on BCF v3 Localization | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Total Graph / Embedding Snapshots** | `127` `.json` / `127` `.npz` | Loaded in memory by harness; not guaranteed mounted inside `/workspace`. | `MEASURED` |
| **Node Schema Keys** | `['id', 'name', 'text']` | Nodes carry **only** `id`, `name`, and `text`. **No file path and no line numbers** are stored on nodes; `rg -n` is required for line numbers. | `MEASURED` |
| **Edge Schema Types** | `['calls']` | 100% of edges have `type='calls'`. No inheritance, import, or data-flow edges exist in the pre-computed graphs. | `MEASURED` |
| **`async def` Node Coverage** | **`0` across `367,985` nodes** | Every `async def` endpoint or coroutine (`21–23` tasks) is completely absent from the AST graph. `rg -n 'async def '` is mandatory. | `MEASURED` |
| **Reachability Class Distribution (`GRAPH-1`..`4`)** | `GRAPH-1`: `120` (`93.02%`), `GRAPH-2`: `4` (`3.10%`), `GRAPH-3`: `4` (`3.10%`), `GRAPH-4`: `1` (`0.78%`) | `9` tasks (`6.98%`) modify module-level constants, non-indexed template files (`rich/_export_format.py`, `docs_src/`), or brand-new files (`scripts/prepare_release.py`). | `MEASURED` |

---

## 4. Empirical DSA Benchmarks & Mathematical Formulations

### 4.1 Disjoint Interval Binary Search (`bisect_right`) vs. Linear Scan (`DSA-04`)

In `Textualize/rich` (`rich/cells.py`, `CHAIN-10`), terminal cell widths are resolved against sorted `(start_cp, end_cp, width)` interval tables. Our live CPython benchmark (`empirical_results.json`) across `50,000` lookups on `400` intervals measures:
- **Linear scan (`O(M)`):** `1.14008 s`
- **`bisect.bisect_right` (`O(log M)`):** `0.02439 s` (**`46.75x` speedup**, `MEASURED`).

### 4.2 Harsanyi / Möbius Set-Function Interaction Index for Multi-Hunk Patches (`DSA-08`)

Across the 129 benchmark tasks, `55.04%` (`71/129`) require `>= 2` hunks and `29.46%` (`38/129`) touch `>= 2` files. For a reference patch decomposed into hunk units $U = \{u_1, \dots, u_k\}$ and a test outcome indicator $P_t(S) \in \{0, 1\}$ for subset $S \subseteq U$, the Möbius interaction index is:

$$\Delta_t(S) = \sum_{T \subseteq S} (-1)^{\lvert S \rvert - \lvert T \rvert} P_t(T)$$

- **Additive ($\Delta_t(\{i,j\}) = 0$):** One hunk fixes test $t$ independently.
- **Conjunctive / Masked ($\Delta_t(\{i,j\}) = +1$, where $P_t(\varnothing)=P_t(\{i\})=P_t(\{j\})=0$ and $P_t(\{i,j\})=1$):** Neither hunk shows test progress alone (e.g. symmetric sync/async twins in `httpx_3672` or producer+consumer hunks in `fastapi_15745`). An agent that reverts every edit that does not immediately turn a test green will fail all conjunctive tasks; hence BCF v3 uses **Crash-Fork + Sibling Twin Search + Post-Fix Re-Probing**.

---

## 5. Per-Algorithm Operational Guide

### 5.1 `DSA-01`: Call-Graph / Import-Graph k-Hop Ego-Network Traversal & Node Centrality
- **Competition Grounding:** Harness loads `graphs/<repo>_<commit>.json` into NetworkX with node keys (`id`, `name`, `text`) and directed edges (`type='calls'`). 93.02% (120/129) of tasks are `GRAPH-1` (callable symbol in graph); 3.10% (4) `GRAPH-2`; 3.10% (4) `GRAPH-3` (0 nodes in file); 0.78% (1) `GRAPH-4` (new file). Crucial blind spot: 0 `async def` nodes exist across all 367,985 graph nodes.
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Use `get_code_neighbors(node)` (1 hop, returns both incoming callers and outgoing callees) when a symbol name is known; NEVER pass >3 nodes to `get_code_subgraph(nodes)` on E4B/31B due to vLLM `#753973` `_parse_gemma4_array` infinite loop; fall back immediately to `rg` for `async def`, `GRAPH-2`, `GRAPH-3`, and `GRAPH-4`.

### 5.2 `DSA-02`: Cycle Detection & Topological Sorting on Dependency / Exception / Router Graphs
- **Competition Grounding:** Directly solves `RC-2C` tasks (`rich_3772` cyclic `__cause__`/`__context__`/`exceptions` graph in `Traceback.extract`, `fastapi_14258` `router.include_router(router)` self-inclusion guard) and `RC-1B` unwrapping loops (`fastapi_14448` / `inspect.unwrap` `_id_seen` stopper).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Whenever traversing recursive object graphs (`__cause__`, `__context__`, `__wrapped__`, `partial.func`, sub-routers, or Pydantic recursive schemas), maintain `seen: set[int] = set()` keyed by `id(obj)`.

### 5.3 `DSA-03`: AST Dominance, Control-Flow Guard Analysis & Recursive Schema Tree Rewriting
- **Competition Grounding:** Used in `RC-4A` OpenAPI/JSON Schema `$ref` remapping (`fastapi_14349`, `fastapi_14361`, `fastapi_14246`), `RC-3C` `anyOf` response schema deep copying (`fastapi_14463`), and BCF `Crash Fork` static dominance inspection (determining whether a crash site is dominated by an upstream state producer).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** When inspecting or transforming nested JSON Schema dicts (`properties`, `anyOf`, `allOf`, `$defs`) or AST trees. Always guard recursive dict walkers with `isinstance(v, str)` on `$ref` keys because user model fields can be named `'$ref'` (`fastapi_14349`).

### 5.4 `DSA-04`: Binary Search on Disjoint Code-Point Interval Tables & Monotonic Cell-Cut Convergence
- **Competition Grounding:** Core algorithm of `Textualize/rich` (`rich/cells.py` and `rich/segment.py`, 9 tasks in `Rich-2` / `RC-5A`): binary searching `(start_cp, end_cp, width)` intervals (`rich_3930`, `rich_3944`, `rich_3953`, `rich_4006`) and proportional interpolation search in `Segment._split_cells` (`rich_3506`, `rich_3521`).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** When mapping code points to cell widths or splitting mixed ASCII + double-width CJK strings at column `cut`. Always verify boundary invariants: `cp < table[0][0]`, `cp > table[-1][1]`, zero-width progress (`step >= 1`), and CJK split landing on column `cut - 1` vs `cut`.

### 5.5 `DSA-05`: 1D Half-Open Span Interval Algebra (`_Span(start, end, style)`) & Coordinate Shifting
- **Competition Grounding:** Core data structure of `rich/text.py` (`Text._spans`): half-open `[start, end)` character-offset intervals. Bugs arise when mutating `self._text` (tab expansion in `rich_3061`, control-code stripping in `rich_3471`, self-append in `rich_3480`, or Markdown cell concatenation in `rich_4079`/`rich_3130`) without shifting or copying `[start, end)` coordinates.
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Whenever inserting, deleting, or expanding characters in `rich.text.Text`, recompute `[start + delta_start, end + delta_end)` for every `_Span` or use `Text` methods that maintain span invariants, and copy `text._spans.copy()` when `text is self`.

### 5.6 `DSA-06`: Myers O(ND) Shortest Edit Script vs. Ratcliff/Obershelp `difflib.SequenceMatcher`
- **Competition Grounding:** Governs how `edit_file(path, old_string, new_string)` matches target blocks and how `get_status()` / `submit_patch()` evaluates diffs in Container B. Also explains `TIER-1`..`TIER-5` hunk locality (59.69% of tasks are 1-file `<=35` lines).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Include 3-5 unique context lines around `old_string` in `edit_file` so `str.count(old_string) == 1`; NEVER pass triple backticks inside `edit_file` arguments (`#756051` key-swallowing bug)—use a short python `pathlib` script via `run_command` instead when editing code containing backticks.

### 5.7 `DSA-07`: Thompson NFA vs. Backtracking Regex Engine (`re` / `_sre`) & Zero-Width Lookbehind Splitting
- **Competition Grounding:** Directly underlies `RC-5C` and `RC-2B` tasks: `re.split(r'(?<=\n)', text)` preserving trailing newlines (`rich_4076`), `_ansi_tokenize` matching DEC private sequences `\x1b[0-?]` (`rich_3278`), URL character class `[~@]` in `ReprHighlighter` (`rich_3454`, `rich_3067`), and `_is_single_cell_widths` character range regex (`rich_3535`).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Use zero-width lookbehind `re.split(r'(?<=\n)', s)` when splitting lines while preserving exact newline tokens; test regex changes against empty strings, trailing delimiters, and non-ASCII inputs.

### 5.8 `DSA-08`: Zeller's `ddmin` Delta Debugging & Harsanyi/Möbius Set-Function Interaction Index
- **Competition Grounding:** 55.04% (71/129) of benchmark tasks have `>=2` hunks and 29.46% (38/129) touch `>=2` files; 36 tasks have 2-4 source hunks where exact $2^k$ Möbius interaction $\Delta_t(S)$ is computable to distinguish additive ($\Delta=0$), redundant ($\Delta=-1$), and conjunctive/masked ($\Delta=+1$) multi-hunk fixes.
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** When a multi-hunk fix (`TIER-3`..`TIER-5`) fails tests or causes a regression, bisect hunks or re-probe after each hunk (`BCF-FC` loop) to detect conjunctive twin edits (e.g. `src/httpx/` + `src/ahttpx/` in `httpx_3672`) or unmask downstream faults.

### 5.9 `DSA-09`: Hash Table Invariants (`__hash__` / `__eq__`), `MultiDict` Bucket Lists & Key-Alias Sets
- **Competition Grounding:** Covers `RC-3A`, `RC-3B`, and `RC-3C` hash/dict defects: `Style._hash` cache invalidation when mutating cloned `Style` (`rich_2943`), making `params.Depends` and `Security` hashable (`__hash__` in `fastapi_14372`), caching scopeless sub-dependencies (`fastapi_14419`), and tracking both `field.alias` and `field.name` in `processed_keys: set[str]` (`fastapi_14360`, `fastapi_14371`).
- **Operational Rule (`when_to_use`):** Whenever an object caches its hash (`self._hash`) or is stored as a dict/cache key (`sub_cache_key`), verify that `a == b` implies `hash(a) == hash(b)` and that any cloning/mutation method resets `_hash = None`.
