# WS-11 Dossier: Verification, Dynamic Probing & Static Analysis Toolchain under Air-Gapped Constraints

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-11` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Container Constraint:** `network_mode='none'`, CPython `3.12.13`, 5.5–6.0 min/task budget | **Machine Artifact:** [`verification_tools.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/verification_tools.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary

In the competition's two-container evaluation architecture, **Container A** runs with `network_mode='none'` (zero PyPI access) and a strict `12-hour` wall-clock ceiling across all tasks (`~5.58 min/task`). Furthermore, `11` public tasks fail unrelated tests in the uncalibrated sandbox (`#755554`, `#754586`). Therefore, the agent's verification toolchain must be **(1) zero-install (stdlib + pre-installed `pytest`/`rg`/`git`)**, **(2) sub-second in cold-start latency**, and **(3) surgically scoped (`pytest <file> -k <expr> -x --tb=short -q`)** so it never triggers unrelated baseline failures or context-window compaction (`#754783`).

---

## 2. Verification Tool Latency, Availability & Invocation Matrix (`verification_tools.jsonl`)

| Tool ID | Tool Name | Category | Sandbox Availability | Cold Start (ms) | Per-Call (ms) | Recommended Invocation | Anti-Pattern to Avoid |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | ---: | ---: | :--- | :--- |
| `VT-01` | **pytest Targeted Selector (`pytest <file> -k <expr> -x --tb=short -q`)** | Dynamic Test Execution | Yes (guaranteed across all task containers; Container B uses pytest for `FAIL_TO_PASS` and `PASS_TO_PASS`) | `420` | `650` | `pytest tests/test_target.py -k 'test_keyword' -x --tb=short -q --no-header` | Running full `pytest` across the entire repository (takes 45s-180s in `fastapi` and triggers uncalibrated baseline failures in 11 public tasks) or omitting `-q --tb=short` (floods context window with 5,000+ tokens). |
| `VT-02` | **ripgrep (`rg -n --no-heading`) / `grep -rn` Fast Lexical & Regex Locator** | Static Code Search | Yes (`rg` or `grep` in standard Linux userland inside Container A) | `15` | `35` | `rg -n --no-heading 'def target_symbol\|class TargetClass' fastapi/ tests/` | Using `search_similar_code` with natural-language sentences (returns 0 results or 0.999 noise) or relying solely on `get_code_neighbors` for `async def` functions (0 `async def` nodes exist in the pre-computed AST graphs!). |
| `VT-03` | **Python `ast` + `py_compile` Zero-Dependency Syntax & Symbol Outline Verifier** | Static AST Verification | Yes (CPython 3.12.13 stdlib) | `45` | `60` | `python -m py_compile <modified_file.py>` | Submitting a patch without compiling modified files or checking line numbers via guesswork when `read_file` truncates at 150 lines / 10,000 chars (`#755291`). |
| `VT-04` | **Git Workspace Hygiene & Diff Verifier (`git status --porcelain`, `git diff`)** | Patch Gate & Hygiene Verification | Yes (`git` in Container A plus harness `get_status()` free tool call) | `20` | `30` | `git status --porcelain && git diff --stat` | Leaving untracked repro scripts (`repro.py`) inside `/workspace` instead of `/tmp/repro.py`, or deleting required `docs_src/` tutorial files on `PC-4` tasks (`SR-4`). |
| `VT-05` | **Focused MRE Reproducer Script (`python /tmp/repro.py`)** | Dynamic Failure-Signature Probe | Yes (CPython 3.12.13 in Container A with live editable install of `/workspace`) | `80` | `180` | `python /tmp/repro.py` | Classifying bugs from issue text alone (0/129 prompts contain a Python traceback; `I(file; text_class) <= 0.12` bits) instead of provoking a live failure signature via `/tmp/repro.py` (`BCF-FC` active probe). |
| `VT-06` | **CPython `warnings` & `pytest.warns` Deprecation Verifier** | Deprecation & Diagnostic Verification | Yes (CPython 3.12.13 stdlib + pytest) | `60` | `120` | `python -W error -c 'import fastapi' && pytest -k 'deprecation' -q` | Running standard `python -c 'import ...'` without `-W error` when diagnosing `RC-6A`/`RC-6B` import-time deprecation tasks (`fastapi_14186`, `fastapi_14077`). |
| `VT-07` | **CPython `sys.monitoring` (PEP 669) / `trace` / `coverage` Lightweight Line Tracer** | Dynamic Coverage & Reachability Probe | Yes (`sys.monitoring` and `trace` in CPython 3.12 stdlib; `coverage` optional) | `90` | `220` | `python -c "import sys; ..." (or `pytest --tb=short` frame inspection)` | Using `sys.settrace` across full test suites (10x+ overhead) when a 10-line `sys.monitoring` or traceback frame inspection suffices. |
| `VT-08` | **Static Type & Lint Checkers (`mypy`, `ruff`) — Offline Availability Caveat** | Static Type / Lint Analysis | Repo-dependent (not guaranteed in all task containers; Container A has `network_mode='none'`) | `800` | `1200` | `which ruff && ruff check <modified_file.py> (optional; never block on missing linter)` | Assuming `mypy` or `hypothesis` is pre-installed in every task container or attempting `pip install` inside `network_mode='none'`. |

---

## 3. Four-Gate Verification Hierarchy (`V0` → `V3`)

| Gate ID | Gate Name | Exact Command Executed via `run_command` | Budget Cost | Pass Criterion |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`V0`** | **Syntax & Import Gate** | `python -m py_compile <modified_files.py>` | `< 100 ms` | Exit code `0`; catches E4B QAT trailing-character drops (`#753277`) and indentation errors immediately. |
| **`V1`** | **Active Failure-Signature Reproducer (`BCF-FC`)** | `python /tmp/repro.py` (or `pytest <test_file.py> -k '<new_case>' -x --tb=short -q`) | `200–700 ms` | Fails *before* edit with target signature; exits `0` *after* edit. |
| **`V2`** | **Module Regression & Unmasking Sweep** | `pytest <affected_test_file.py> -x --tb=short -q --no-header` | `0.6–2.5 s` | 100% pass across all tests in the affected test module (`PASS_TO_PASS` proxy + unmasking check). |
| **`V3`** | **Workspace Hygiene & Tutorial Gate (`SR-4`)** | `git status --porcelain && git diff --stat` (+ `pytest tests/test_tutorial/<topic>/ -q` if `PC-4`) | `< 100 ms` | Zero untracked scratch files in `/workspace`; required new modules/tutorials (`GRAPH-4`, `PC-4`) intentionally tracked; free `submit_patch()` called. |

# WS-12 Dossier: Batonic Coding Framework (BCF) v3 Brain Architecture — Audit, Math & Declarative Card Library

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-12` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Baseline Audited:** `bcf/BCF-Reference-Compendium.md`, `04_Building_the_BCF_Brain_and_Contest_Submission_Blueprint.md`, `2026-10-13_BCF_Brain_MultiSource_Harvest_v2`, `3-cc-opus55` | **Machine Artifact:** [`bcf_design_elements.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/bcf_design_elements.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary: Extending & Challenging the Prior BCF Specification (`CQ-02`)

Per `CQ-02`, Workstream `WS-12` audits every core principle and card of the prior Batonic Coding Framework (BCF) specifications against the 129-task empirical dataset and the 8 competition execution killers, classifying each element as **`Confirmed`**, **`Revised`**, or **`Refuted`**. Crucially:
1. **Refuted — Issue-Text Exception Routing (`BCF-PRINCIPLE-02`):** `0/129` problem statements contain a Python traceback (`MEASURED`), and issue-text classes carry `<= 0.12` bits about the fix file (`p = 0.076`), worsening leave-one-out localization rank from `5.84` to `6.57`. BCF v3 replaces issue-text classification with **Active Failure-Signature Probing (`BCF-FC`)**: provoke a live failure via `/tmp/repro.py` or `pytest`, then classify the observed failure signature.
2. **Refuted — Graph-First Natural-Language & Subgraph Protocol (`BCF-PRINCIPLE-03`):** `search_similar_code` matches symbol keys (not sentences), `get_code_subgraph` triggers the fatal vLLM `#753973` infinite loop, and `graphs/*.json` contain `0` `async def` nodes. BCF v3 replaces this with **`rg -n` Lexical-First + 1-Hop Sync `get_code_neighbors` Corroboration (`SR-5`)**.
3. **Revised — Multi-Agent `SequentialAgent` & Custom Code Gates (`BCF-PRINCIPLE-04`):** Custom Python tools are disallowed in YAML-only submissions, and `SequentialAgent` triggers ADK bug `#754636` while `LlmEventSummarizer` triggers `#754783`. BCF v3 uses a **Single Flat `LlmAgent` Topology** with raised compaction thresholds (`24k–28k` tokens) and compact inline declarative cards.
4. **Extended — 12-Card Library (`DC-01`..`DC-12`):** Extends the 10-card `v2` harvest with **`DC-11` (ANSI/Markup/Line-Break Tokenization & Escape Sanitization)** and **`DC-12` (Deprecation Lifecycle & Fast-Path Serialization)**, achieving **100% coverage of all 106 taxonomic groups**.

---

## 2. Complete Audit Ledger of Prior BCF Principles & Declarative Cards (`bcf_design_elements.jsonl`)

| Element ID | Prior Source Spec | Element Title | Audit Verdict | Empirical Rationale & Competition Grounding | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-01` | Compendium A1 / 04_Building_the_BCF_Brain §1 | **Four Core Tenets (Preserve Working Code, Minimum Path, Breadcrumbs, Binary Done)** | **`Confirmed`** | 59.69% (77/129) of benchmark tasks are `TIER-1` or `TIER-2` single-file surgical patches (`<=35` lines). Minimal surgical edits and binary test verification directly prevent collateral `PASS_TO_PASS` regressions. | `MEASURED` |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-02` | Dossier Turn 20 / Primer App. B-C | **Issue-Text Exception-Class Routing (Phase 1a from Problem Statement Text)** | **`Refuted`** | Measured across all 129 public tasks: **0/129** problem statements contain a Python traceback (`Traceback (most recent call last)`), only 15/129 name an exception class, and issue-text classes carry `<=0.12` bits of mutual information about the fix file (`p = 0.076` against within-repo permutation null), worsening leave-one-out localization rank from 5.84 to 6.57. Replaced by **Active Failure-Signature Probing (`BCF-FC`)**: provoke the failure via reproduction/pytest first, then classify the observed failure signature. | `MEASURED` |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-03` | Whitepaper Draft 2 §4.1-4.3 | **Graph-First Natural-Language Dual Queries & `called_by` / `max_depth` Arguments** | **`Refuted`** | 1) `search_similar_code(query)` matches symbol keys, not natural-language sentences; 2) `get_code_neighbors(node)` has no `depth` parameter and all edges have `type='calls'`; 3) `get_code_subgraph(nodes)` has no `max_depth` or `edge_types` parameters and triggers the fatal vLLM `#753973` `_parse_gemma4_array` infinite loop on multi-element arrays; 4) AST graphs have **0 `async def` nodes** across 367,985 nodes and 72% isolated nodes in `fastapi`. Revised to **Hybrid `rg` + 1-Hop `get_code_neighbors` Corroboration (`SR-5`)**. | `MEASURED` |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-04` | Compendium A6.4 / A8 | **Code-Enforced Custom ADK Gate Tools (`write_spec`) & Multi-Agent `SequentialAgent` Topology** | **`Revised`** | Custom Python tools are forbidden in YAML-only submissions, and multi-agent `SequentialAgent` triggers ADK bug `#754636` (continuation nudge routes back to `sub_agents[0]`, duplicating 10k-12k tokens) while ADK `LlmEventSummarizer` triggers `#754783` (wiping 100% of tool call/response history at 14,336 tokens). Revised to **Single Flat `LlmAgent` Topology** with prompt-enforced phase gates and deterministic `/tmp` shell verification. | `MEASURED` |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-05` | 3-cc-opus55 Task 2 §B5 & §C3 | **Reorder-Never-Prune Bayesian Search Rule & Post-Fix Re-Probing for Masked/Conjunctive Faults** | **`Confirmed`** | Hard pruning caps success probability at classifier fidelity $f$, whereas posterior reordering achieves the $S(K,f) = 1/(1/K + 1 - f)$ speedup (and $K\cdot f$ budgeted multiplier) with zero coverage loss. Re-probing after each edit (`BCF-FC`) detects conjunctive multi-hunk fixes ($\Delta_t(S) = +1$, 55% of tasks have `>=2` hunks) and unmasked downstream faults. | `DERIVED` |
| `BCF-PRINCIPLE-06` | 2026-10-13_BCF_Brain_MultiSource_Harvest_v2 (`SR-4`) | **Universal Untracked File Deletion (`git clean -fd`) Before `submit_patch`** | **`Revised`** | While scratch scripts must never pollute `git diff`, blindly deleting all new files breaks `GRAPH-4` net-new module synthesis (`fastapi_15661` `scripts/prepare_release.py`, `fastapi_15030` `fastapi/sse.py`, `rich_3930` `rich/_unicode_data/*`) and `PC-4` tutorial-coupled tasks (10 tasks where Phase 2 `tests/test_tutorial/` imports newly created `docs_src/` files). Revised to **Scratch-in-`/tmp` + Intentional `git add` for Required New Modules/Tutorials**. | `MEASURED` |
| `DC-01` | BCF Brain MultiSource Harvest v2 + Extended v3 | **Type Coercion, PEP 604/695 Unpacking & UTF-8 Byte Normalization** | **`Confirmed`** | Covers `RC-1A`, `RC-1B`, and `RC-4B` (28 tasks): unwrapping `Annotated`, `(Union, types.UnionType)`, and `TypeAliasType.__value__`, plus UTF-8 byte length calculation (`len(s.encode('utf-8'))`) and `has_read(obj)` protocol fallback. | `MEASURED` |
| `DC-02` | BCF Brain MultiSource Harvest v2 + Extended v3 | **Static/Dynamic Attribute, Identity (`is param.empty`), & `NameError` Guard** | **`Confirmed`** | Covers `RC-1B`, `RC-1C`, and `RC-2A` (19 tasks): guarding `inspect.signature(call, eval_str=True)` with `except (NameError, TypeError):`, checking `param.default is param.empty` (never `==`), and guarding uninitialized `@dataclass` `init=False` fields. | `MEASURED` |
| `DC-03` | BCF Brain MultiSource Harvest v2 + Extended v3 | **Exception Hierarchy, Cycle Guard (`_visited: set[int]`), & HTTP Status Normalization** | **`Confirmed`** | Covers `RC-2C`, `RC-4B`, and `RC-6A` (16 tasks): cycle detection via `id(exc)` / `router is self`, RFC 9110 `401 Unauthorized` vs `403 Forbidden`, `FastAPIDeprecationWarning(UserWarning)`, and `BrokenPipeError` devnull redirection. | `MEASURED` |

---

## 3. Information-Theoretic Foundation of `BCF-FC` (Fault-Class Search)

Let $\Theta$ be the set of candidate code locations in a repository ($\lvert\Theta\rvert = 4,263$ in `fastapi`, $2,015$ in `rich`, $759$ in `requests`, $740$ in `httpx`) and $L \in \Theta$ be the true fault location with prior $\pi$. By Arikan's (1996) guesswork bound, the expected number of inspections $G(\pi) = \sum_{i \ge 1} i p_{(i)}$ satisfies:

$$\frac{\left(\sum_i \sqrt{p_i}\right)^2}{1 + \ln \lvert\Theta\rvert} \le G(\pi) \le 2^{H_{1/2}(\pi)}$$

When the agent executes an active probe (`python /tmp/repro.py` or `pytest`) at cost $c_r$, it observes a live failure signature $Y$ (exception type, innermost repository frame, assertion diff) mapped to class $\hat{C} \in \{1, \dots, K\}$ with fidelity $f$:
- **Unbounded Search Speedup (`Result 1`):** $S(K, f) \approx \frac{1}{1/K + (1 - f)}$ (capped at $\frac{1}{1-f}$ regardless of $K$).
- **Budget-Limited Success Multiplier (`Result 2`, Competition Regime $B \le N/K$):** $P_{\text{class}}(\text{found}) = (K \cdot f) \cdot P_{\text{none}}(\text{found})$.
- **Category Split Criterion (`Result 3`):** Splitting $K \to K'$ improves search iff $\frac{1}{K} - \frac{1}{K'} > f - f'$.
- **Reorder-Never-Prune Rule (`Result 4`):** Hard pruning caps resolve rate at $f$; posterior reordering preserves 100% of the $S(K,f)$ speedup with zero coverage loss.

---

## 4. The 8 BCF v3 Strategic Rules (`SR-1`..`SR-8`)

| Rule ID | Strategic Rule Name | Operational Directive | Mitigated Failure / Killer |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`SR-1`** | **Active Repro & Module-Wide Re-Probe** | Provoke the failure via `/tmp/repro.py` or `pytest` before editing; after editing, re-run both the reproducer and the full target `tests/test_*.py` module to detect unmasking (`BCF-FC`). | Symptom-only overfitting & conjunctive multi-hunk masking (`DSA-08`). |
| **`SR-2`** | **Crash-Fork Default: Fix State Producer First** | When an exception crashes inside a consumer/helper, inspect the upstream caller/state producer 1 hop up before adding a local guard at the crash site. | Plausible-but-incorrect symptom guards failing hidden `PASS_TO_PASS` tests. |
| **`SR-3`** | **2-Strike Circuit Breaker & Clean Revert** | If `2` consecutive edits fail with the identical test signature, immediately run `git checkout -- <file>` and switch to the secondary hypothesis. | Runaway 50-turn edit loops & ADK compaction wipeout (`#754783`). |
| **`SR-4`** | **Scratch-in-`/tmp` & `PC-4`/`GRAPH-4` Tracked Hygiene** | Write all repro/helper scripts in `/tmp/repro.py` (never `/workspace`); before `submit_patch`, verify `git status --porcelain` is clean except for intentional `GRAPH-4` modules or `PC-4` `docs_src/` tutorials. | Untracked file pollution in `git diff` vs missing `docs_src/` `ModuleNotFoundError`. |
| **`SR-5`** | **Lexical `rg -n` First + 1-Hop Sync Graph Corroboration** | Always run `rg -n '<symbol>'` first to get file paths, line numbers, and `async def` symbols; never call `get_code_subgraph` (`#753973`); use `get_code_neighbors` only on verified sync `GRAPH-1` symbols. | vLLM `#753973` infinite hang & 0-`async def` AST graph blind spot. |
| **`SR-6`** | **Backtick-Free Editing & Chunked Reads** | Never pass triple backticks to `edit_file` (`#756051`; use a `python -c` `pathlib` write instead); always bound `read_file` to `<= 150` lines (`<= 100` on E4B, `#755291`). | `edit_file` 51-call loop (`#756051`) & silent `read_file` truncation (`#755291`). |
| **`SR-7`** | **Sibling / Twin Call-Site Sweep** | After fixing a defect at one location, run `rg -n` for identical patterns in the same file or sync/async twin package (`src/httpx/` + `src/ahttpx/`, `_compat/v2.py`, `docs_src/*_py310.py`). | Incomplete multi-hunk patches (`TIER-3`..`TIER-5`, `55%` of benchmark). |
| **`SR-8`** | **75% Budget Submit Safeguard** | Upon receiving `budget_warning` or reaching `75%` of turn/time budget with a syntax-valid (`py_compile`) partial fix that passes existing tests, clean `/workspace` and call `submit_patch()` immediately. | Unsubmitted valid patches lost to hard wall-clock timeout. |

# WS-13 Dossier: Complete 106-Group Per-Subcategory Playbook Library (`playbooks.jsonl`)

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-13` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Total Playbooks:** `106` (`92` with `distinct_api=True`, `14` process/structural playbooks with `distinct_api=False` per `CQ-06`) | **Machine Artifact:** [`playbooks.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/playbooks.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Fit-Decision Architecture

Every taxonomic group across all 10 dimensions (`106` groups total) has a complete, machine-verifiable operational playbook in `playbooks.jsonl` and in Section 2 below. To respect context-window and instruction-adherence limits (`1,850` tokens on 31B, `950` tokens on E4B), each playbook carries an explicit `fit_decision` (`check_card_fit_decisions.py`):
- **`in_card` (`56` groups):** High-frequency, repo-agnostic triage patterns compiled directly into the compact `DC-01`..`DC-12` and `SR-1`..`SR-8` tables in the root `agent.yaml` instruction.
- **`in_skill_resource` (`46` groups):** Detailed subsystem, lifecycle, and layout playbooks stored as modular ADK skill resources (`skills/bcf_playbooks/resources/*.md`) loaded on-demand via `load_skill_resource`.
- **`exclude` (`4` groups: `RC-7B`, `CHAIN-01`, `CHAIN-03`, `CHAIN-09`):** Historical PR-saga forensics or one-off maintainer script patterns documented for human analysis and offline training curation, but excluded from the runtime agent prompt to prevent public-set overfitting.

---

# WS-14 Dossier: Two-Track Model Tailoring & Gated QLoRA Roadmap (`31B` vs. `E4B`)

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-14` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Staff Single-Variant Rule (`#750016`):** Strictly enforced (two separate submission packages built from one shared BCF v3 source tree) | **Machine Profiles:** [`variant_profile_31b.yaml`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/variant_profile_31b.yaml), [`variant_profile_e4b.yaml`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/variant_profile_e4b.yaml).

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Two-Slot Final Submission Strategy (`CQ-04`)

Kaggle allows **two final submissions** for private leaderboard evaluation (`HARNESS_README.md` / `#751104`), and competition staff (`#750016`) confirmed that mixing model variants within a single submission is prohibited. Therefore, the optimal portfolio allocates:
- **Final Slot 1 — Track A (`gemma-4-31b-it-qat-w4a16-ct`):** Targets the **`$125,000` Main Leaderboard** (`Rank 1 Yurnero = 0.29` [`17/58`], `Ranks 2–10 = 0.24` [`14/58`]).
- **Final Slot 2 — Track B (`gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct`):** Targets the **`$10,000` Small Model Bonus (`#751104`)** awarded to the highest Private LB score using `E2B` or `E4B` among a team's 2 selected final submissions.

---

## 2. Side-by-Side Parameter & Policy Matrix (`variant_profile_31b.yaml` vs. `variant_profile_e4b.yaml`)

| Configuration Dimension | Track A: Gemma 4 31B (`gemma-4-31b-it-qat-w4a16-ct`) | Track B: Gemma 4 E4B (`gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct`) | Technical Justification & Grounding | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Prize Target** | Main Leaderboard (`$125,000` Top-5 Pool) | Small Model Bonus (`$10,000`, `#751104`) | Orthogonal prize maximization across the 2 final slots. | `DOCUMENTED` |
| **vLLM TP & Decode Speed** | `TP=2`, `17.25 tok/s` decode (`1.71 s` prefill) | `TP=1`, `~48.0 tok/s` decode (`~0.62 s` prefill) | `#755291` measured 31B decode bottleneck (`76%` of wall-clock). | `MEASURED` / `DERIVED` |
| **`max_time_minutes`** | `6.0 min` (Base QAT) / `5.5 min` (with LoRA) | `4.5 min` (Base QAT & LoRA) | Prevents 12-hour (`720 min`) global timeout under LoRA `+29%` latency inflation (`#755677`). | `REPORTED` / `DERIVED` |
| **`max_turns` / `max_tool_calls`** | `40` turns / `25` tool calls | `28` turns / `18` tool calls | Keeps trajectories below ADK compaction cliff (`#754783`) and stops E4B context drift. | `DERIVED` |
| **`compaction_threshold_tokens`** | `28,000` tokens | `24,000` tokens | Overrides default `14,336` threshold where `LlmEventSummarizer` wipes tool history (`#754783`). | `REPORTED` |
| **`temperature` / `thinking_budget`** | `0.05` / `2048` tokens (`3072` base max) | `0.0` (greedy) / `1024` tokens | `temp=0.0` minimizes E4B QAT trailing-character drops (`#753277`); `thinking_budget=2048` offsets LoRA `+45%` token inflation (`#755677`). | `REPORTED` |
| **Agent Topology** | Single flat `LlmAgent` (`use_sub_agents: false`) | Single flat `LlmAgent` (`use_sub_agents: false`) | Eliminates ADK `SequentialAgent` continuation bug (`#754636`) on both tracks. | `REPORTED` |
| **System Prompt Token Budget** | `<= 1,850` tokens (`12` cards `DC-01`..`12` + `8` rules) | `<= 950` tokens (`8` top cards + `6` core rules) | Small models lose instruction adherence when system prompt exceeds `~1,000` tokens. | `DERIVED` |
| **`read_file` Max Lines** | `150` lines (`start_line`..`end_line` required) | `100` lines (`start_line`..`end_line` required) | Matches harness silent truncation ceiling (`150` lines / `10,000` chars, `#755291`). | `REPORTED` |
| **Graph Tool Policy** | `get_code_neighbors` allowed (1-hop sync only); `get_code_subgraph` **FORBIDDEN** | **All graph tools FORBIDDEN** (`rg -n` only) | Prevents vLLM `#753973` `_parse_gemma4_array` OOM freeze and E4B tool format errors (`#752128`). | `REPORTED` |

---

## 3. Gated Phase-4 QLoRA / SFT Training Protocol & `#755677` Fix (`CQ-07`)

Rank 1 (`Yurnero`, `0.29` Public LB) confirmed that LoRA fine-tuning is viable and provides the edge over `0.24` scaffold-only entries, provided two critical engineering blockers from Thread `#755677` (Tarek Hamdi) are resolved:

### 3.1 Blocker 1: Unwrapping `Gemma4ClippableLinear` Before `get_peft_model()`

In `transformers`, Gemma 4 QAT checkpoints wrap linear layers (`q_proj`, `k_proj`, `v_proj`, `o_proj`, `gate_proj`, `up_proj`, `down_proj`) inside `Gemma4ClippableLinear`, causing stock `peft.get_peft_model()` to fail with `ValueError: Target module Gemma4ClippableLinear is not supported`. Before applying PEFT, unwrap `.linear` in-place:

```python
import torch.nn as nn
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model

def unwrap_gemma4_clippable_linears(model: nn.Module) -> int:
    replaced = 0
    for parent_name, parent_mod in list(model.named_modules()):
        for child_name, child_mod in list(parent_mod.named_children()):
            if child_mod.__class__.__name__ == 'Gemma4ClippableLinear' and hasattr(child_mod, 'linear'):
                setattr(parent_mod, child_name, child_mod.linear)
                replaced += 1
    return replaced
```

### 3.2 Blocker 2: Compensating for Active LoRA Token (`+45%`) & Latency (`+29%`) Inflation

When vLLM serves an unmerged LoRA adapter alongside the W4A16-CT base weights, per-task token generation increases by `+45%` and wall-clock time increases by `+29%` (`#755677`). Without tightening the runtime budget, a LoRA submission hits the 12-hour hard timeout and scores `0.00`. Both `variant_profile_31b.yaml` (`max_time_minutes: 5.5`, `thinking_budget: 2048`) and `variant_profile_e4b.yaml` (`max_time_minutes: 4.5`, `thinking_budget: 1024`) bake in this exact compensation.


---

# WS-15 Dossier: Gemma 4 E4B (`gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct`) Readiness, Failure Modes & `$10,000` Bonus Strategy

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-15` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Target Model:** `gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct` (`4.5B` effective parameters) | **Target Prize:** `$10,000` Small Model Bonus (`#751104`).

---

## 1. Executive Summary: Why E4B Is the Highest-ROI Asymmetry in the Competition

While `90.99%` of teams (`4,375/4,808`) are stuck at `0.00` debugging 31B timeouts and the remaining `9%` are competing for 5 main-track prizes with 31B/26B, Google's **`$10,000` Small Model Bonus (`#751104`)** rewards the single highest Private LB score achieved using **`gemma-4-e4b`** (or `e2b`) among a team's 2 selected final submissions. Because `gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct` runs at `TP=1` with `~2.8x` higher token throughput than 31B, it is immune to the 31B decode-speed starvation—provided its **three E4B-specific quantization and parser failure modes** are neutralized.

---

## 2. E4B Specific Failure Mode Matrix & Deterministic Guards

| Risk ID | E4B Failure Mode | Root Cause & Thread Reference | Observable Symptom in Trace | Mandatory BCF-Lite E4B Guard (`variant_profile_e4b.yaml`) | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`E4B-R1`** | **QAT 4-Bit Trailing Character Drop (`#753277`)** | 4-bit W4A16-CT weight quantization induces slight logit drift at string-closing quote tokens, dropping the last character of `old_string`, `new_string`, or `path`. | `edit_file` fails with `old_string not found` or introduces a `SyntaxError` on the last line of the replacement block. | 1) Set `temperature: 0.0` (greedy); 2) Keep `edit_file` hunks `<= 15` lines; 3) Run `python -m py_compile <file> && git diff` immediately after every edit; 4) Fall back to python `pathlib` replacement script on first `edit_file` failure. | `REPORTED` |
| **`E4B-R2`** | **vLLM `_parse_gemma4_array` Infinite Hang (`#753973`)** | E4B frequently emits unescaped `]` or malformed list syntax when calling `get_code_subgraph(nodes: list[str])`, triggering an infinite `while` loop in vLLM 0.19.1 (`26.7 GB` RAM spike). | vLLM server hangs permanently; all subsequent tasks fail with connection timeout (`0.00` overall score). | **Completely disable `get_code_subgraph`, `get_code_neighbors`, and `search_similar_code`** in E4B prompt (`allow_get_code_*: false`); use `run_command` with `rg -n` exclusively (all string args, zero array args). | `REPORTED` |
| **`E4B-R3`** | **Tool Call Format Corruption (`#752128`, `#756051`)** | Long system prompts (`>1,500` tokens) or triple backticks inside `edit_file` cause E4B to emit raw markdown tool blocks or swallow `,old_string:`. | Tool call is emitted as plain text (triggering ADK continuation nudges) or repeats `edit_file` 50 times. | Cap E4B system prompt at `<= 950` tokens (`8` core cards, `6` rules), cap `thinking_budget: 1024`, and strictly forbid backticks inside `edit_file`. | `REPORTED` |
| **`E4B-R4`** | **Multi-File / Architectural Horizon Drift (`TIER-4`/`5`)** | With `4.5B` effective parameters, E4B degrades when tracking `>3` open files across `>20` turns. | Incomplete multi-file refactors breaking `PASS_TO_PASS` tests (`resolved = 0`). | Focus E4B on surgical `TIER-1` (`30.23%`), `TIER-2` (`29.46%`), and `TIER-3` (`21.71%`) tasks (`81.40%` of corpus); enforce strict 2-strike `git checkout -- .` abort on `TIER-5` tasks so E4B never submits a broken partial refactor. | `DERIVED` |

---

## 3. E4B Expected Solve-Rate Ceiling by Taxonomic Tier & Trajectory Distillation Plan

| Complexity Tier | Share of Benchmark | 31B Target Solve Rate | E4B Zero-Shot (BCF-Lite) Target | E4B + Distilled QLoRA Target | E4B Tactical Directive |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | :--- |
| **`TIER-1` (1 file, 1 hunk, `<=6` lines)** | `39/129` (`30.23%`) | `55%–65%` | `35%–45%` | `45%–55%` | Primary E4B scoring engine: `rg -n` → `read_file(100 lines)` → `/tmp/repro.py` → `edit_file` → `py_compile` + `pytest` → `submit_patch`. |
| **`TIER-2` (1 file, 1–3 hunks, `<=35` lines)** | `38/129` (`29.46%`) | `40%–50%` | `20%–30%` | `30%–40%` | High viability when guided by `DC-01`..`DC-10` idioms and `BCF-FC` active reproducer. |
| **`TIER-3` (1–2 files, `<=120` lines)** | `28/129` (`21.71%`) | `25%–35%` | `10%–18%` | `18%–25%` | Require `SR-7` sibling `rg` check after first hunk; abort on 2nd consecutive test regression. |
| **`TIER-4` & `TIER-5` (`>2` files or `>120` lines)** | `24/129` (`18.60%`) | `10%–20%` | `0%–5%` | `5%–10%` | Fast-fail after `180s` if `V2` module tests do not pass; preserve wall-clock budget for `TIER-1..3`. |
| **Weighted Overall Public/Private Target** | **`129/129` (`100%`)** | **`0.31–0.38`** | **`0.18–0.24`** | **`0.24–0.31`** | **Competitive to win `$10,000` Small Model Bonus (`#751104`) outright.** |

# WS-16 Dossier: Cross-Workstream Synthesis, Contradiction Log, Evidence Gap Ledger & Red-Team Audit

> **Workstream ID:** `WS-16` | **Sweep Date:** `2026-11-05` | **Machine Artifacts:** [`contradiction_log.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/contradiction_log.jsonl), [`evidence_gap_ledger.jsonl`](file:///d:/stuff/ai-misc/_bsh2026/vscode_project01/101e-Kaggle-antigrav/deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Python_Triage_and_BCF_Brain_Roadmap/evidence_gap_ledger.jsonl).

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Workstream `WS-16` synthesizes the findings of `WS-01` through `WS-15`, documents all resolved source contradictions (`CL-01`..`CL-07`), records remaining evidence gaps with concrete closing experiments (`GAP-01`..`GAP-04`), subjects the BCF v3 roadmap to a hostile red-team audit, and records the execution results (including mandatory negative controls) of all 10 automated verification scripts (`§12.3` of the handoff prompt).

---

| Contradiction ID | Topic | Source A Claim | Source B Claim | Authoritative Resolution | Evidence Tier |

| `CL-01` | **Axis 2B Root-Cause Sub-Category Count (22 vs 25)** | deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Problem_Dataset_Taxonomy_and_Analysis/02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md Section 1 table (line 17) & 00_README.md (line 61): states '22 (RC-1A to RC-8B)'. | deliverables/2026-11-05_Gemma4_Problem_Dataset_Taxonomy_and_Analysis/02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md Section 3.2 (lines 86-110) & 05_Quality_Check_and_Table_Validation_Report.md Table 2.1: explicitly enumerates 25 distinct RC-* subcategories summing to 129. | Resolved in favor of Source B (25 distinct RC-* subcategories: RC-1A..1C [3], RC-2A..2C [3], RC-3A..3D [4], RC-4A..4C [3], RC-5A..5D [4], RC-6A..6C [3], RC-7A..7C [3], RC-8A..8B [2] = 25). The '22' in Section 1 was a copy-paste clerical error from the task count of RC-1 (22 tasks). | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-02` | **Total Taxonomic Group Count (97 vs 105 vs 106 vs 235)** | 02_Multi_Dimensional_Problem_Taxonomy_and_Subcategories.md line 3 headline states '48 primary + 57 sub = 105', while its Section 1 table rows sum to 36 primary + 61 sub = 97. | 05_Quality_Check_and_Table_Validation_Report.md Table 2.1 rows 1-10 sum to 6+17+8+25+18+5+5+4+8+10 = 106 groups (and row 11 adds the 129-task catalog = 235 table rows). | Resolved in favor of 106 distinct taxonomic groups across the 10 dimensions (Axis 1A: 6, Axis 1B: 17, Axis 2A: 8, Axis 2B: 25, Axis 3: 18, Axis 4: 5, Axis 5A: 5, Axis 5B: 4, Axis 6: 8, Chains: 10). Frozen in `taxonomy_baseline.json`. | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-03` | **Leading Double-Slash (`//`) URI Path Normalization (`requests_6644` vs `requests_7315`)** | Task `requests_6644` (PR #6644, 2024-02-23): collapses leading `//` in `HTTPAdapter.request_url` via `url = f'/{url.lstrip("/")}'` to prevent `urllib3.util.parse_url('//v:h')` authority confusion. | Task `requests_7315` (PR #7315, 2026-05-10): deletes those exact two lines because collapsing `//` breaks AWS S3 SigV4 presigned URLs whose object keys begin with `/`. | Both tasks are in the 129-task benchmark (`CHAIN-01`) at different `base_commit` snapshots! The agent must inspect the current state of `src/requests/adapters.py` and the task prompt (`PC-3`) rather than memorizing a static snapshot of `adapters.py`. | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-04` | **Markdown Table Inline Span Styling (`rich_3130` vs `rich_4079`)** | Task `rich_3130` (PR #3130): added `TableDataElement.on_text` in `rich/markdown.py` calling `text.stylize(style)` to preserve links/bold in table cells. | Task `rich_4079` (PR #4079): found that `rich_3130`'s `text.stylize` call clobbered child inline code spans inside Markdown table cells, replacing it with non-clobbering span composition. | Demonstrates why `DC-09` / `RC-5B` requires non-clobbering `Style` composition (`base_style + child_span.style`) rather than post-hoc `stylize()` overwrites. | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-05` | **Issue-Text Exception Classification vs. Reproduced Failure-Signature Classification** | BCF Dossier Turn 20 / Primer Appendix B-C: proposes classifying tasks into exception classes from the issue description text in Phase 1a. | Empirical measurement on all 129 tasks (`3-cc-opus55` Task 2 §D): 0/129 problem statements contain a Python traceback, only 15/129 name an exception class, and issue-text classes carry `<=0.12` bits about the fix file (`p = 0.076`), worsening LOO localization from 5.84 to 6.57. | Issue-text exception routing is refuted and replaced by `BCF-FC` Active Failure-Signature Probing: run `/tmp/repro.py` or targeted `pytest` first, then classify the live failure signature. | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-06` | **Pre-Computed AST Graph Utility vs. 0-Async-Node & vLLM Array Hang Reality** | BCF Whitepaper Draft 2 §4 & Harness Overview: promote `get_code_subgraph`, `get_code_neighbors`, and `search_similar_code` as the primary localization layer. | Empirical measurement (`04-deep-dive-critical-technical-findings.md` & `#753973`): 1) `get_code_subgraph(nodes)` triggers an infinite loop in vLLM 0.19.1 `_parse_gemma4_array` on unescaped `]`; 2) AST graphs contain `0` `async def` nodes across 367,985 nodes; 3) `fastapi` graphs have 72% isolated nodes; 4) nodes lack file paths and line numbers. | Forbid `get_code_subgraph` and natural-language `search_similar_code`; use `rg -n` as the primary locator and 1-hop `get_code_neighbors(symbol)` only as a secondary caller/callee check on sync `GRAPH-1` symbols (`SR-5`). | `MEASURED` |
| `CL-07` | **CPython 3.11 vs 3.12 `@runtime_checkable` `Protocol` Behavior on `__getattr__` Proxies** | Local Windows CPython 3.11.9 empirical run (`empirical_results.json`): `isinstance(GetattrFileProxy(RealReader()), SupportsRead)` returns `True` because Python 3.11 uses `hasattr`. | Scorer Container CPython 3.12.13 (`gh-102433`, `requests_7433`/`7502`/`7505`): `typing._ProtocolMeta.__instancecheck__` uses `inspect.getattr_static`, which returns `None` on `GetattrFileProxy` and causes `isinstance(..., SupportsRead)` to return `False`. | All runtime protocol checks on file-like or iterable objects in CPython 3.12+ must pair `isinstance(obj, Protocol)` with `or hasattr(obj, 'read')` (`def has_read(obj)` in `requests_7505`). | `MEASURED` |

## 3. Evidence Gap Ledger & Closing Experiments (`evidence_gap_ledger.jsonl`)

| Gap ID | Workstream | Unresolved Question / Gap Description | Potential Impact | Concrete Closing Experiment / Mitigation | Status | Evidence Tier |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `GAP-01` | `WS-01 / WS-14` | Exact composition of the ~120 private evaluation tasks (repositories, issue distribution, and exact task count) is held in secret by Kaggle/Google until competition close. | High — over-tuning on `fastapi`/`rich`/`requests`/`httpx` file paths will fail to transfer if private tasks come from unseen Python repositories. | Enforce repo-agnostic transfer hypotheses on all 106 groups (`check_group_coverage.py`) and evaluate all prompt/skill changes on a held-out pseudo-private repo split (`requests` + `httpx` held out when tuning on `fastapi` + `rich`). | **Mitigated by Repo-Agnostic Design** | `DOCUMENTED` |
| `GAP-02` | `WS-11` | Exact per-container pre-installed version list of optional static linters (`mypy`, `ruff`, `hypothesis`, `coverage`) across all 129 task Docker images. | Medium — an agent playbook that hard-depends on `ruff` or `mypy` would fail if a task container only installs `pytest` and library runtime dependencies. | Gate all optional third-party verifiers behind `which ruff` / `python -c 'import coverage'` and rely strictly on CPython 3.12.13 stdlib (`py_compile`, `ast`, `warnings`, `sys.monitoring`) + `pytest` + `git` + `rg` for core verification. | **Closed by Stdlib+Pytest Fallback Policy** | `DOCUMENTED` |
| `GAP-03` | `WS-10 / WS-12` | Full $2^k$ subset execution (`Interference Atlas` Experiment E2) across all 36 public tasks with 2-4 source hunks in Container B requires ~340-800 Docker test runs. | Medium — provides exact empirical fraction of conjunctive ($\Delta_t(S) = +1$) vs additive ($\Delta_t(S) = 0$) multi-hunk patches for the Paper Track writeup. | Run CPU-only `ddmin` / subset runner (`E2` in `02_BCF_Fault_Class_Math_Whitepaper_Content.md`) across the 36 2-to-4-hunk tasks before the 2026-11-12 Paper Track deadline. | **Scheduled for Paper Track Execution** | `HYPOTHESIS` |
| `GAP-04` | `WS-14 / WS-15` | Exact public and private LB score of `gemma-4-e4b-it-qat-w4a16-ct` with the BCF v3 Lite scaffold + distilled QLoRA adapter on the 58-task public LB. | High — determines the winning margin for the $10,000 Small Model Bonus (`#751104`). | Execute Track B Milestone M2 (zero-shot E4B BCF-Lite submission) followed by Milestone M4 (E4B QLoRA distilled from verified 31B passing trajectories) and record paired DEV-24 and Public LB resolve rates. | **Actionable in Track B Roadmap** | `HYPOTHESIS` |

## 4. Hostile Red-Team Audit of the BCF v3 Architecture & Roadmap

| Red-Team Attack Vector | Hostile Critique | BCF v3 Architectural Countermeasure | Residual Risk |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **RT-01: Public-to-Private Overfitting (`CQ-08`)** | 'Your 106-group taxonomy was derived from `fastapi`, `rich`, `requests`, and `httpx`. If the 120 private tasks come from `django`, `click`, `sqlalchemy`, or `aiohttp`, repo-specific rules will fail.' | 1) Every `in_card` rule (`DC-01`..`DC-12`, `SR-1`..`SR-8`) is phrased strictly in terms of **CPython 3.10–3.14 stdlib primitives and IETF/Unicode protocols**, never hardcoding repo file names; 2) Repo-specific historical chains (`CHAIN-01/03/09`, `RC-7B`) are marked `fit_decision='exclude'`; 3) All prompt ablations are gated on a held-out pseudo-private repo split. | **Low** |
| **RT-02: Prompt Bloat Degrading Small Model Tool Calling** | 'Putting 106 playbooks into the system prompt will exceed 15,000 tokens, causing E4B and 31B to hallucinate tool arguments and hit compaction on Turn 1.' | Progressive skill disclosure (`WS-12`/`WS-13`): only compact `in_card` summaries (`<=1,850` tokens on 31B; `<=950` tokens on E4B) reside in `agent.yaml`; the `46` detailed `in_skill_resource` playbooks load on-demand via `load_skill_resource`. | **Low** |
| **RT-03: Silent Timeout on Unmerged LoRA Adapters (`#755677`)** | 'Adding a QLoRA adapter increases token output by +45% and latency by +29%, which will push a 6-minute per-task run over the 12-hour global limit and score 0.00.' | Both variant profiles (`variant_profile_31b.yaml` and `variant_profile_e4b.yaml`) explicitly tighten `max_time_minutes` (`5.5` min on 31B LoRA; `4.5` min on E4B LoRA) and cap `thinking_budget` (`2048` on 31B; `1024` on E4B). | **Low** |
| **RT-04: Single Unescaped Bracket Freezing vLLM (`#753973`)** | 'Even one call to `get_code_subgraph` with an unescaped `]` in the node list freezes vLLM 0.19.1 across all remaining tasks.' | `allow_get_code_subgraph: false` in both 31B and E4B profiles; on E4B all graph tools are disabled in favor of `rg -n` via `run_command`. | **Eliminated** |
| **RT-05: Uncalibrated Sandbox Failing Tests (`#755554`)** | 'If the agent runs `pytest` without arguments on `fastapi` or `requests`, it will see pre-existing environment failures and waste all 25 tool calls trying to fix unrelated tests.' | `VT-01` and `SR-1` forbid bare `pytest`; the agent always runs `pytest <specific_test_file.py> -k '<keyword>' -x --tb=short -q`. | **Low** |
